# Trapped in the Internet: the Oct 31 crunch plan

**The party is Saturday, October 31, 2026. Today is Thursday, October 15: 16 calendar days.**
This is the **active plan**. [`SCHEDULE.md`](./SCHEDULE.md) is the full roadmap (245.5 dev-days) for
after the party and the next run. [`CHECKLIST.md`](./CHECKLIST.md) tags every item 🎃 (in for Oct 31),
🩹 (runs on a workaround below), ⭐ (optional) or 💤 (after). The story spec is
[`DESIGN.md`](./DESIGN.md) (§7 has the workarounds the story already uses), and the runbook + cue sheet
is [`README.md`](./README.md).

> **The scope rule.** A feature is in for Oct 31 only if it is **merged and verified on a real device
> (a real phone, tablet or the TV computer, over the VPS) by Tue Oct 27, 18:00**. Anything that isn't
> is **cut to its fallback or workaround, never half-shipped**: revert it or leave it switched off.
> After Oct 27: fixes only. After Oct 30: no code at all.

Built 2026-10-15 from three passes: a triage of all 67 WPs, a hardware and production plan for Halifax,
and a show-runner walk-through of the whole night against this scope. Decisions applied: #25 yes (push to
phones; wall tablets, the TV and the chess phone send a heartbeat that alerts the director instead). #33
yes (a DM lie counts only when filed through a "📝 File a fact" card). Spec sign-offs #1–#12 are all
approved. #28 is answered: Sandy is **lore only** (her codex entries + her one line inside the song tracks), with an **optional cutscene** (a short pre-rendered video on the TV, P2) — no prop, no live voice. Owner: `@__`.

**Revised the same evening after two critic passes** (a capacity review checked against the code, and a
show-safety review of the night). Changes:

- **Estimates corrected.** K-31 is split into K-31a (Android + booths) and K-31b (iPhone, conditional). K-16, N-08 and K-28 went up. K-13 went down.
- **New items.** K-32 (VPS load test), N-10 (release a performer's part), and safety edits inside K-05 and K-08.
- **Story rule fixed.** The old "never Push draft after 10-25" rule would have shipped the 10-25 story. It is replaced by evening syncs and a morning-of Push draft (§3).
- **Show-safety fixes.** The blackout no longer reaches the stairs or the server-room route. The maglock rules are deleted. Strobe presets drop to ≤ 3 Hz. The chess AI is set so it can be beaten.
- **Crew and procedures.** A house manager, an `ALL STOP` call word, and procedures for late arrivals and lost performers.

Decision **#35** (whether Trabolta may fall back to a hosted model, §12) is answered: **no** — the directors answer his DMs and N-11 posts his hold line (§5 *Wave 1 status*).

**Wave 4 (built 2026-10-16, committed as 9d35a4b, not deployed):** the user's 18 asks of that day — invitation codex packages, team-authored facts (`facts.json`), the server judge + the bottle, the Truthcoin wallet, staff quest logs, the house announcements and the Installation Wizard in place of the old voices, marked for reinstallation, the King — are in §5 *Wave 4 status*, with the decisions taken on the user's behalf, the open issues and the deploy order. The open questions wave 3 left are answered there and in place.

**Wave 5 (built 2026-10-16, committed as 85d2bad + a35f175, not deployed):** the stock-take of every item still open after wave 4, built in eight lanes — the wallet's loose ends and Runescape's reserve (grant + ten-minute freeze), the story's open promises (Truth Pong cups, DraftKings' majority, Jeeves' ten findings, MS Paint's painting, the `!!` takeover, the resurrection, Bathroom Mode timing out), the editor's Run cards (Wallet, House map, award suggestions, who filed what), the director's phone cue sheet, the terminals' Trust Chart reveal + identify, stagehand's lights catch-up, and K-31b built dormant. It is in §5 *Wave 5 status*, with the decisions taken on the user's behalf, the deferred list and the open issues.

**Wave 6 (built 2026-10-17 on branches `oct31-w6/*`, merged to `master`, not deployed):** the 23 distinct bugs four live test runs found (door, wallet, hunt, screens) and the open items that are cheap and make a promise true, in eight lanes — server, play, story, content, screens, reinstall, stagehand, ops-docs. It is in §5 *Wave 6 status*, with the decisions (MS Paint's invitation is canon), the not-building list and the deploy order. **The VPS still runs `w3-1015`: the first release with wave 6 ships waves 4, 5 and 6 together.**

**Wave 7 (built 2026-10-17 on branches `oct31-w7/*`, merged to `master`, not deployed):** the server-room door gets a real **standalone keypad lock** (the user's call). A program learns the code by winning at chess against the King (or the chess phone), by a riddle, or with the majority; the code is a clean entry, so the door never alarms and only the second wrong patch-bay combination does; the door opening plays **The Heart**, a cutscene in the server room; a **server-room terminal** lets a program talk to Trabolta candidly, "like the Master in Fallout 1", and talk him down (🕊, human-confirmed). Plus wave 6's open issues 2–6. Five lanes — platform, story, agent, hardware, terminal — then integration and docs. It is in §5 *Wave 7 status*, with the decisions taken for the user, the deploy order and the open issues. Shipped as `w7-1017` the same day (Deploy log).

**Wave 8 (built 2026-10-17 on branches `oct31-w8/*`, merged to `master`, shipped as `w8-1018` on 10-18):** with every committed build item done, wave 8 drove the whole show on scratch servers again, wave 6's way: nine live testers (the night doors → ceremony, every ending, crash / restart recovery, the booths, the phone UI, the screens, the editor's Run mode, Trabolta's pipeline, load) plus a stock-take and a docs ↔ code pass; independent verifiers kept 72 of 83 findings. One was a **blocker**: a restart with the doors closed (a paused preview's Push draft, sync-show reload or Go live in place) never played Power On, so the night had no combos, door code or defaults. Six fix lanes, an integration round (the cue sheet's NEXT now follows tonight's ending), a recheck that re-ran every tester's driver on the merged tree, and two docs lanes. It is in §5 *Wave 8 status*, with the decisions taken for the user, the deploy order and the open issues.

**Wave 9 (built 2026-10-18 on branches `oct31-w9/*`, merged to `master` as ae79117, shipped as `w9-1018` and checked live):** a second find on the shipped show — fourteen testers (the wallet, the judge and the bottle, the reinstallation games, the control plane on Postgres, the crew cards followed literally, an adversarial guest, a whole night in real browsers, the stagehand contract, every timer across pause and restart, the featured sheets, regression reviews of wave 8, the open items, ops) and a verifier per finding; all 53 findings stood. Nine fix lanes, two integration rounds and this docs pass: phones, booths and Run reconnect by themselves after a restart (Caddy holds requests 30 s; `/api/health`; the boot waits for a late database), the cue sheet is quiet while a timer owns the night and refuses a cue pressed a step early, McAfee referees and holds no pot, 💡 holds the dance, the Judging card overrides any filing, `sync-show` never reloads a live show with real guests, and a director console costs 0.12 Mbit/s instead of 6. The agent hub's stall handling was reverted before shipping and parked on `oct31-w9/agent-stall`. It is in §5 *Wave 9 status*, with the decisions taken for the user (D1–D10, veto any), the deploy order and the open issues.

**Wave 10 — the Director page (built 2026-10-18 on branches `oct31-w10/director-{core,show,editor,phone}`, merged as 3eac8de, shipped as `w10-1018`):** Run → Director is rebuilt around the question *what do I press next, and is it safe?* The show's admin cues are grouped by **act** (eight `act:` lines: B, C, D, E, F, G plus Anytime; no cue moved), a **NOW card** shows NEXT with its description and explains an empty NEXT with **countdowns** (the header's `countdown:` lines), every cue on the laptop and the phone is **armed, then fired** (a fast double-click only arms; an arm drops when the cues move), a refused press shows **Not taken** with the `# hosts` reason, and a glance column (Needs you, the header's `watch:` lines with the Kernel masked, People, Cast, the `# hosts` feed) replaces hunting through World. It is in §5 *Wave 10 status*.

**Wave 11 — the King, the cold door and the workarounds (built 2026-10-18 on branches `oct31-w11/*`, merged to `master`, shipped as `w11-1019` on 10-19):** the user's design of that day. The door code comes **one digit per QR note** (four secret notes hidden in four rooms; all four assemble *The Door Code*), from **a checkmate against the King** or from the majority, as before; the riddle is no longer a path. The King plays chess **only on the Server Door terminal** outside the server-room door, awake all night, with no hints, and in **both reinstallation games** he has **one shared 15-point health bar for the night**: one hit per program per visit, his bar sets his strength at the door, and at 0 he kneels for everyone. **The Heart** plays on the first door opening once Trabolta is awake, however the door was unlocked, and the heart terminal takes codex files. Ghosts wear the guest's **profile picture**. Plus three workaround bundles: 📷 Scan + the wrong-browser landing, guest countdowns and reserved featured programs; near-miss judging, the unset slider and a present-only majority; arm-then-fire booth buttons, the server-room Antivirus' own fallbacks, Windows Defender at the door and Trabolta's busy line + stall detection. It is in §5 *Wave 11 status*, with the decisions taken for the user, the deploy order and the open issues.

**Wave 11b — Trabolta judges (built 2026-10-19 on branches `oct31-w11b/*`, merged to `master`, shipped as `w11b-1019` the same night):** the user's answers after the wave 11 live check, *"do we really need Directors to judge everything? Can't Trabolta judge things?"* and *"marked programs can use the terminal, but Trabolta should change what he says / thinks of them, still letting them continue"*. The header's `judge: Trabolta` puts every `auto`-mode **near miss** to his worker for one stateless ruling (*🤖 Trabolta ruled …* in `# hosts`; a director overrides only to disagree; with no worker online wave 11's rule stands). At his heart a **marked** program sees him notice the mark and ask why, and carries on. Plus two live fixes: the **door code moved** (the old one was also a year in a public bio) and a reserved program's short name (*Excel*) is held. It is in §5 *Wave 11b status*.

**Wave 13 — the Play app UX overhaul (built 2026-10-20 on branches `oct31-w13/*`, merged into `oct31-w13/integration`, not yet merged to `master` or shipped):** the user's seven asks of that day about the participant app: noisy rooms, a slow typewriter, a bloated top bar with the pass and the scanner out of reach, pop-ups that get buried, too many buttons per scan, a host panel that never leaves the app, and a long Codex / Quest Log. The phone is now the 1990s computer the guests are trapped in: a **NOW title bar** on top (the story's current objective, authored as the new `OBJECTIVE`), a **Win95 taskbar** at the bottom (⊞ Start, 🎟 Pass and 📷 Scan one tap from anywhere, a tray with the story's clock, the balance and missed moments), rooms that only crawl what arrives while you watch, a card tray and folded notices, and an interrupt **ladder** (balloon → sticky → dialog → blue screen) the story or a host switches live. Booths open a guest's card at the scan with at most three buttons, and **Task Manager, Adobe Acrobat and the Recycling Bin are hosts** (`admin: true`): a NEXT bar and a five-tab console in their own booth. It is in §5 *Wave 13 status*, with the decisions taken for the user, the deploy order and the open issues.

## 3. How the lanes run

**Since 2026-10-15** waves 1 and 2 are merged to `master` (wave 1's lane branches + worktrees are retired); wave 3 shipped as `w3-1015`, wave 4 (9d35a4b) and wave 5 (85d2bad) are committed, and waves 6–9 ran on `oct31-wN/*` branches and are merged (§5); waves 4–8 shipped (`w6-1017`, `w7-1017`, `w8-1018`), and wave 9 shipped as `w9-1018`. What's left (fixes from the device checks, K-31b if it's a go, the optional items) lands on `master` directly; the redeploy + sync after each merge still apply. The rules below are how wave 1 ran.

- One lane = one Claude Code session in its own git worktree, one package context, **merged to `master` every evening** after you review it. Every item ends with its acceptance check **on a real device against the VPS**, not only `vitest`.
- Lanes keep running the suites (`pnpm -r test`, `uv run pytest`, `loomcheck --smoke`) before each merge. Lane D owns the deploy, so it redeploys the VPS after each evening merge.
- **You** review, run the device checks, and run the hardware and production lane (§8) with friends. If four lanes are too many to review, fold lane D into C after 10-20: the Quest and the rig bench mostly wait on hardware anyway.
- **The device hour.** Every evening, one fixed hour for device checks. Each item ships a scripted acceptance check (Playwright mobile, `curl`, or `scripts/accept/<id>`), so your part on the device is ≤ 10 minutes. Lane D's checks all need your hands (K-02, K-03, the K-30 sideload, the K-05 bench, K-28 data entry), so lane D gets code-only work on your heavy hardware days (10-17, 10-18). The 10-22 bench is the one exception, since it is the gate.
- **Merge conflicts.** `core/server/event-runtime.ts` (≈ 3,000 lines) is touched by K-11, K-13, N-02, N-10, K-31a/b, K-16, K-18, K-22, K-26 and K-29: all four lanes. The editor's Run/Director/Inspector code is touched by K-12, K-21, K-06, N-02, N-10 and K-29. So:
  - each feature's routes live in their own `core/server/routes/<id>.ts`, with a one-line dispatch in `event-runtime.ts`;
  - the evening merge order is fixed at **B → A → C → D**, and each lane rebases before handing in.
- **Getting the story onto the VPS.** `core/scripts/sync-example.ts` runs with `DATABASE_URL` over an SSH tunnel to the VPS's Postgres and `--server https://app.<domain>`. `scripts/sync-show.sh` (K-01, on master) wraps it as one script — `LOOM_SSH=<user>@app.mapsandducks.com scripts/sync-show.sh` (`--dry-run` prints the plan, `--no-reload` skips the restart) — and it runs after each evening merge. **Since wave 9 (D6) it never reloads a live event with a real guest on its roster** (or one whose roster it can't read): it writes the files, leaves the event alone and exits 3; `--i-know-this-is-live` (or `LOOM_I_KNOW_THIS_IS_LIVE=1`) overrides. A preview always reloads. Since wave 2 it also pushes `house.json` + `house.shed.json`, but **the project's copy wins**: after a survey, Save to project on the map page (the sync keeps it); to make the repo's copy the show's again, Download JSON, commit it as `house.json`, then sync with `--replace-maps`.
- **The show runs the story you Push, not the one you write.** Go live restarts from the running snapshot (`events-api.ts:216`, `runtime.restart(undefined, …)`), so a fix that was never pushed never reaches guests. The rule:
  - **Evenings, 10-16 → 10-30:** after the merge, sync + **Push draft** on the preview (lane D). It wipes rehearsal guests only. Performers re-sign after Go live anyway.
  - **Never during a run-through, the tech or the dress** while friends are playing (OP-03).
  - **Morning of 10-31:** sync, Push draft, check the door QR on cellular, then **Go live in place**, all before anyone registers (W-01).
  - **After doors:** never Restart, Push draft, Go live or End.

## 4. Day by day, Oct 15 → Nov 1

Item ids are in §5 (scope + acceptance). "✔" marks what must be checked on a device that day.

**Wave 1 ran ahead of this table:** on 10-15 every committed build in the lane columns was merged to `master` except K-02 / K-03 / K-04 / K-07 (device runs, no new code), K-28 (waits on the 10-17 survey) and K-31b (conditional on the 10-21 push check). So a lane cell from 10-16 on now means **that item's device check on that day** (§5 *Wave 1 status*, `scripts/accept/<id>`), and the freed lane-days are buffer for the fixes they turn up. The "You + friends" and Gate columns stand as written. **Since wave 5** (2026-10-16, working tree) K-31b is built too, dormant behind `LOOM_HOMESCREEN`: lane B's 10-21 → 10-23 K-31b cells are now the go / no-go, turning it on, and `scripts/accept/k31b.sh`, not a build. N-04a (the TV's mp4) is prebuilt and needs only the file, and the K-32 coalescing fix for the 10-24 buffer is built and off (`LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS`).

## 5. The build list (what actually gets built)

Each line: lane · lane-days · done by · the minimal scope (anything not named is out) · **the acceptance check on a real device**. The checklist items each closes are tagged `🎃 <id>` in CHECKLIST.md.

### Wave 1 status (merged to `master` 2026-10-15, not yet deployed to the VPS)

Lanes merged in order B → A → C → D, then `fix(oct31): integrate wave 1` (a530814), the verification pass (5ff3b8b) and the leftovers (d58803e) — all on `master` (ba494a7..d58803e). Suites on `master` after the leftovers: loomcheck `--smoke` 30 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings; core 877 **with** the Postgres-gated tests (`loom_dev`); house 125; reinstall/web 120; terminal 124; play 91; editor 201; bank 142; stagehand 230; ops scripts 98 (`scripts/ops/test/run.sh`); live-guard 2; server-room firmware 112 host checks + an ESP32 compile and reinstall/vr 226 + quest.sh 26 (both as integrated, not re-run).

**Facts this wave was built on (they override the plan above where they differ):**
- **One house Mac** (the 48 GB Ollama laptop) is "the TV computer" everywhere in this plan. It drives the TV over the long HDMI run (`/terminal/?…&view=tv` in Chrome `--kiosk --autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required`), the speakers through the MOTU (ADAT; the house announcements on channels 1–4 = Z1 living + Z2 kitchen), and runs Mosquitto + Zigbee2MQTT, stagehand `show` and `agents` (separate launchd jobs), the `alexa` module (the house-announcement player: it keeps that code name in the YAML, `scripts/house-mac/alexa.md` and the launchd label, but since wave 4 nothing in the fiction answers to that name) and Ollama with `OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=2` (both models stay loaded; OP-06's "mind paused by default" is no longer forced).
- **Decision #35 = no hosted fallback.** If Trabolta's worker dies the server posts his `hold:` line after 20 s (N-11) and the directors answer his DMs from Run. If the house internet dies, the cues are fired by hand (QLab, the Z2M frontend, WLED presets, `stagehand announce` / `mosquitto_pub -t show/announce`).
- **The VPS is live** at https://app.mapsandducks.com (app, `/edit/`, API, apex invite site); invitations are sent; the PAUTIX strips are addressable on an off-the-shelf WLED controller (presets; preset 22 = dim Tube during a blackout); Meta developer mode is on.
- **Broker layout (integration decision):** Mosquitto's anonymous listener is bound to `127.0.0.1:1883` (stagehand, Z2M, the `alexa` module), and the network props (WLED controllers, the server-room ESP32) use a password-only LAN listener `:1884` as user `props` — `scripts/house-mac/alexa.md` → *Lock down the broker*; the ESP32 reads `SR_MQTT_USER` / `SR_MQTT_PASS` from `secrets.h`.

**Before any device check: deploy wave 1 to the VPS** (it is on `master`; the VPS still runs the pre-wave-1 build). In the VPS `.env`: `LOOM_BASE_URL=https://app.mapsandducks.com`; `LOOM_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` / `LOOM_VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` (`npx web-push generate-vapid-keys`, once, never changed again); ~~`KOKORO_CPUS`~~ (not needed since wave 2: leave `LOOM_TTS_URL` blank or `off`; see *Wave 2 status*, whose deploy steps supersede these); `LOOM_RL_RESOLVE=600`, `LOOM_RL_REGISTER=400`, `LOOM_RL_LOGIN_FAILURES=100/300`. Then `deploy/release.sh --adopt pre-k08`, `docker compose up -d --build`, and `LOOM_SSH=<user>@app.mapsandducks.com scripts/sync-show.sh`.

**Found during wave 1 — fixed in `fix(oct31): wave 1 leftovers`** (each with a test): any ending now stops every story clock (`when Night.ended == true:` clears the hunt / hunt 2 / summons / bingo / Y2K countdowns, so no corner countdown or late time-up beat after `trabolta_disabled`, `computer_destroyed` or any other ending); the play Codex box shows "Too many tries — wait a minute, then try again." on the K-11 429; the Data Centre Sinkhole codex (and the Excel / OpenOffice identity texts + featured sheets) no longer say "five hours early", and a test checks no non-hunt codex entry carries an H1–H6 answer; `chess lost` / `chess drawn` rules (from the Y2K hour, while locked, Trabolta gloats in The Server Room; nothing disarms) and the *The Cold Lock* codex now exists (code-less, handed out with The Y2K Clock at the final round, kept out of Trabolta's first-24 ground truth); the ceremony plays `ceremony_bed` (start) + `award_fanfare` (every award) with QLab rules in `stagehand/trapped.house.yaml`, pinned by `test_the_ceremony_sounds_are_cued`; a `room` / `at` / `location` / `place` argument from an agent power or Run's `mod/signal` folds onto the declared LOCATION (`core/server/place-args.ts`), so `cut_the_lights` with "the start bar" works ("the cache" before the wave 3 renames); `docs/trapped/push-ceremony-dm-lies.md` describes the Home Screen handoff as the single-use `pair=` code flow.

**Dev note (not a behaviour change):** with `DATABASE_URL` unset the server falls back to the local `loom_dev` Postgres (`core/server/config.ts`, a dev convenience), so a scratch server started from a worktree reads and writes the same database as your dev stack. For scratch runs, point `DATABASE_URL` at a dead port (e.g. `DATABASE_URL=postgres://localhost:1/none`) **and, since wave 9, add `LOOM_DB_WAIT=off`** — the control plane then stays disabled and the event plane still runs. (Without it a wave 9 server keeps retrying the database: `/api/health` stays 503 and every unknown code answers 503 `starting`.)

**Verification pass (2026-10-15, `fix(oct31): wave 1 verification`).** Two reviewers (a code read + a full-night drive of Sim / EventRuntime) found no auth bypass, leak, XSS or CSP regression; these were fixed, each with a regression test:
- **N-02 re-pair is a single-use pair code now**, not a guest token in the URL: `/api/mod/guest/pair` → `?code=<event>&pair=<code>` (ten minutes, one use, a newer QR voids the older, cut by a restart); the phone trades it at `POST /api/guest/resume {pair}` for a fresh token (410 when spent / expired; throttled like a sign-in). A guest can no longer hand their own token to someone else's phone, and a phone already signed in asks *Switch this phone* / *Stay <name>* first. `n02.sh` step 4 also checks that the same QR fails on a second phone.
- **Directors answering for Trabolta (decision #35):** a Run `say` as an agent into a guest's DM closes that thread's queued request (or cancels it on a slow worker), so a returning worker no longer answers the same question twice; the guest's next line opens a fresh request with the director's answer in its history.
- **The kitchen no longer comes back on over an ending:** the 90 s `cut_the_lights` restore only fires if `Night.lights` is still the look he cut into (`Night.dark_scene`); any lighting cue since (blackout, dance, alarm, restore) wins.
- **Refused powers don't spend their limit:** a `who: agent` fire the story answers with words alone (wrong room, "not available yet") comes back `refused: true` and isn't counted — the night keeps both blackouts.
- **Push:** a 300-device cap across the event (new devices refused, never evicting), any 4xx but 429 prunes a device, and a guest can't subscribe while the doors are closed (409).
- **Acceptance scripts:** `K-17.sh --fire`, `K-18.sh --say/--beat/--fire/--mute` and `K-20.sh story` run the live guard first (`guard.sh` → `guard-cli.ts`: refuses real guests unless `--i-know-this-is-live`, y/N unless `--yes`); `K-16/17/18` look without a terminal name, so they no longer leave silent rows on the K-29 card.
- **House Mac `say`:** announcement text goes after `--` (a line starting `-o/…` can't write a file).
- **K-10:** Run's empty state has **New performer + moderator codes** (launch `rotate: "staff"`, guest code kept) — tick it before the show if a mod code was shared at rehearsal or typed into a terminal (re-provision terminals with the new code).
- **Minor:** a chess win or `server_unlock` clears a latched alarm at once (no stale overlay); after the night ends no fact is filed, dealt or judged (the ceremony's Truthcoin stands); a crafted guest DM to an agent still hidden from them is refused (403), not stored.

Suites after the pass (before the leftovers; the current numbers are at the top of this section): core 872 + typecheck; play 90 + typecheck; editor 201 + `tsc -b`; stagehand 229.


### Wave 2 status (merged to `master` 2026-10-15, not yet deployed to the VPS)

Five lanes, each with its own review-fix round, merged in order **show-map** (e28da4d) → **map-server** (47ded70) → **map-editor** (9a0556f) → **voices** (4bd3913) → **extras** (9e0d0dd); the commits are `git log --oneline 13e06e9..9e0d0dd`. **Suites as each lane reported them in its own worktree** (per lane, not yet re-run on the integrated `master`; the integration pass reports the final counts): core 895 (show-map) / 904 (map-server, incl. the Postgres-gated tests) / 879 (voices) / 882 (extras), each up from wave 1's 877; house 133 + 3 skipped; reinstall/web 175 (map-editor) / 130 (map-server); terminal 148; editor 208; stagehand 233; ops scripts 103; the accept `burst.test.ts` cases (`node --test`, extras); reinstall/vr 227/0 + `quest.sh` 26/0, run on **both** map variants. **Integrated `master` (after the integration pass + the cross-lane fixes, d0d6e11..8b05894):** core 933 incl. the Postgres-gated tests + typecheck; house 135 + 3 skipped; reinstall/web 193 + tsc + build; terminal 150 + tsc + build; play 91; editor 208 + `tsc -b`; bank 142; stagehand 233; ops scripts 103; accept helpers 10; loomcheck `--smoke` 30 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings; reinstall/vr 231/0 + `quest.sh` 26/0. Live on a scratch server with a throwaway project: drawn room + door + yard cut, Tent → Shed → Tent with a player mid-session, Save to project → End → relaunch plays the saved map (`source: project`), a terminal defaulting to the device voice.

**The user's decision this wave (2026-10-15): native device voices.** *"We can just use the native screen reader voices on the devices? The TTS seems complicated."* So the wall terminals and the TV speak with each device's own voice (`speechSynthesis` → Fully Kiosk's JavaScript Interface `fully.textToSpeech` → the transcript at reading pace, every engine time-limited so a voiceless device never stalls), the house Mac's `alexa` module (house announcements) uses the Mac's own `say`, and **Kokoro is opt-in** (compose profile `tts`; `LOOM_TTS_URL` defaults to `off`, so `/api/mod/tts` answers 503 and a terminal set to the server voice latches to its own). K-19 and N-12 leave the show's path; CHECKLIST SW-AUD-08 is dropped. **That makes K-02 matter more:** a tablet with no browser voice and no Fully TTS only shows text.

**Deploy waves 1 + 2 to the VPS before any device check** (this replaces the wave 1 steps above where they differ):
1. **`.env`:** the wave 1 knobs (`LOOM_BASE_URL`, the VAPID keys, `LOOM_RL_*`), but **no `KOKORO_CPUS` and no `COMPOSE_PROFILES=tts`**; leave `LOOM_TTS_URL` blank or `off` (if it says `http://kokoro:8880/v1`, set it to `off` first).
2. **Ship the server before the first `sync-show` that carries maps** (`scripts/ops/predeploy.sh` → `scripts/ops/ship.sh <tag>`; the first time `deploy/release.sh --adopt pre-k08` on the VPS). An older server joins every project file into the story source, so it would read `house.json` as story text on its next launch. The image build also refreshes the offline map copies bundled into `terminal` + `reinstall/web` (SW-SCR-22).
3. **One-time Kokoro cleanup** (wave 1 bundled it on by default; it holds ≈ 2 GB of RAM until removed): `docker compose rm -sf kokoro`, `docker compose up -d --remove-orphans`, `docker compose ps` shows db / loom / caddy only; optionally `docker image rm ghcr.io/remsky/kokoro-fastapi-cpu:v0.9.0` (`docs/loom-docker-deploy.md` → *The voice*). `scripts/accept/K-19.sh` then prints SKIP and exits 0.
4. **Delete any stale `spatial-house.json`** in the `loom-state` volume (`/data/spatial-house.json` for the default event, `/data/<eventId>/spatial-house.json` for the rest): a director's save outranks the project's map, so an old one hides the new placeholder. `GET /api/spatial/house` reports `source: event` while one is in force. (Later, without a shell: a save survives Push draft and Go live in place, so the map page's status line reading "this event's map" means one is in force — **Save to project** keeps it, switching to the other variant and back drops it.)
5. `LOOM_SSH=<user>@app.mapsandducks.com scripts/sync-show.sh`: pushes the story **and** `house.json` + `house.shed.json` into the project (a project map that already differs is kept and both versions printed; `--replace-maps` overwrites, `--keep-maps` skips), then reloads the preview.

It was committed as **9d35a4b** ("wave 4 - first post-rehearsal notes incorporated") and is **not deployed**. **Suites at 9d35a4b:** core 1155 incl. the Postgres-gated tests (`trapped-in-the-internet.test.ts` alone 130); play 141; editor 213; terminal 153; reinstall/web 271; house 151 + 4 skipped; bank 142; stagehand 234; reinstall/vr 255 + `quest.sh` 26 (as lane F reported); loomcheck `--smoke` 31 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings (`SHOW_KINDS` knows the `answer` card); `pnpm --filter @loom/core titi:facts --check` → "5 outputs up to date with facts.json". **Counts pinned by the show test:** codex 177 entries / 90 codes (`codex/9-facts.loom` is generated, 48 entries), quests 23 (3 generated hunt quests + 13 program quests + 7 staff quests), `guestPickable` 31 (9 featured + 22 pool), 8 performer parts, 8 unplayable characters (Installation Wizard, Kernel, Sandy, The Router, The Screensaver, The Tablet, The Uploader, Truther Download), 101 beats, 48 characters, 106 INTERACTIONs, 92 story rules. No `.loom`, `facts.json`, generated file, mind / persona file or `house.json` still carries a retired name or phrase (the smart speaker, the doctor, the deleted pool program, the stairs wording, the certainty tick, the review lock). The stagehand module that plays the house announcements keeps its code name `alexa` (its YAML key, `scripts/house-mac/alexa.md`, `install-alexa.sh`, the launchd label `com.mapsandducks.stagehand-alexa`): a cleanup, not a character — nothing in the fiction answers to that name any more.

**Rehearse the hunt and the wallet on the server project (the preview), never a local folder:** a folder run has no judge (every `fact answered` arrives without a verdict and `# hosts` reads "could not be judged") and no WalletBook (⭐ instead of the Wallet room).

**Deploy: see the Deploy log above (2026-10-16).** The server ships **before** the first `sync-show` that carries `facts.json` or the king maps.

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (2026-10-16, from the critic's defaults; the user was not available — `scratchpad/decisions.md` has the long form):**
- **U1 / U1b** three codex entries per invitee: 1 = who they really are (`about: <program>`, held from clearance), 2–3 = coded facts in The Desktop; the Truther Download text verbatim as a `secret: true` entry; the per-program glitch lines in `beats/malware.loom` stay.
- **U2 / U2b** the hunt answers, the facts and the character facts live in The Desktop's newspapers / encyclopaedias (the room check-in QRs stay put); one team file `facts.json` + the `titi:facts` generator with DO NOT EDIT banners and an up-to-date test.
- **U3** Clippy + the four Antivirus stay performer parts; they get Quest Log / Codex rooms in the booth, `start quest X for <Character>` and a booth widget surface.
- **U4** the house announcements are a system voice, never a character (fiction only): `do announce` is the house system; the stagehand module keeps the YAML key `alexa:` (a cleanup).
- **U5** Task Manager's DMs are answered from Run by the director or by whoever plays him; every guest can DM him from the start; `# task-manager` is revealed at clearance.
- **U6** transfers: `score >= amount`, 1 ≤ amount ≤ 500, integers; the stimulus is minted, Runescape only, ≤ 3 a night; so is a grant to one program (≤ 3 a night); a freeze refuses one program's outgoing coin for 10 min; requests expire after 10 min.
- **U7** a codex trade is a real escrowed offer (buy or sell an entry for coin; accept / decline; the server moves the coin and shares the entry, journaled).
- **U8** the doctor is gone; the Installation Wizard (Julia Tufts) is a voice (`playable: false`, no booth); the tent is in the backyard through a tunnel.
- **U9** rounds stay 3 / 3 / 2 (H1–H6, F1–F2); the six-mark mechanic is retired.
- **U10** a server-side judge from `facts.json`; `Night.judge_mode` `auto` | `review` (default `auto`), switched from Run → Mind's Judging card; the hosts' 📝 buttons are the override; the verdict never reaches the worker; no "one at a time" lock.
- **U11** consensus = every filed answer matches the key **and** every confidence is 100.
- **U12 / U13** the bottle: a cue picks a random cleared program not yet picked this question, wherever they are in the house; Trabolta asks on the TV; a Truth / Dare card; Dare → marked; Truth → the next personal question; 🗣 / 🙅 in Clippy's booth; "five rounds" = five picks per hunt question.
- **U14** the verdict (Song vs Grok Prom) counts only answered questions: all accepted answers accurate → Song; any lie on record → Grok Prom.
- **U15** the King can be fought any time; a win fires `chess won`, which keeps its Y2K gate (before it the in-game door opens as practice). *(Wave 11: no gate; the board is the Server Door terminal and a win gives the code until 📄.)*
- **U16** a tablet-only board; VR shows the King with a "play it on the tablet" notice. *(Wave 11: no board in either game; both hit his shared bar.)*
- ~~**U17** the wall chess phone stays as the fallback until the King is device-checked.~~ *Retired in wave 11: the chess game is only on the Server Door terminal; ♟ / `chess_won` stay the fallback.*
- **U18** after truth.txt Trabolta DMs everyone for a pickup line (an `answer` card) in both branches.
- **U19** a marked program keeps DMs + The Tube / The Internet, loses buttons, filings, check-in and room posting, and sees one pinned 🚨 card; `captured` is unchanged; the Antivirus get a `!` alert + a `# quarantine` task.
- **U20** ~20 personal questions drafted in `facts.json` `truths` from the team's 4 samples (TD05–TD20 `draft: true`).
- **U21** wave 4 is inside the Oct 27 scope rule; lane order codex/facts → judging/UX → Truthcoin → staff → marked → king; the King is first to cut (§13).
- **12 / 17 / 18** (the user's own asks, applied as written): per-round cards + the round-close rule; *Start Menu* deleted; the tent in the backyard through a tunnel.

**Open issues (every lane, condensed; the long form is the stage-3 summary):**
1. *Still open after wave 5:* `uncertain` filings are marked only in the `truth_1` / `truth_pong` phases: a guess filed for F1 / F2 at truth.txt is neither credited nor marked — confirm "no escorts during the verdict" is intended.
2. *Closed in wave 4:* loomcheck's `SHOW_KINDS` gained `answer` (`core/scripts/loomcheck.ts:127`); `--smoke` is 0 errors / 0 warnings.
3. A local-folder rehearsal has no judge and no WalletBook: rehearse the hunt and the wallet on the server project.
4. Deploy order: the server before the first `sync-show` carrying `facts.json` or the king maps; then the stale kokoro / `spatial-house.json` cleanup (Deploy log).
5. `facts.json` is DRAFT where it says so: the 8 hunt questions and the truths TD05–TD20 are placeholders; the character facts quote the invitations' real-world details — the user's call before printing (SCHEDULE.md, the authoring tasks). The hunt-answer marker is never wrapped.
6. Residual `alexa` identifiers (the module key, the scripts, the launchd label) stay per U4; renaming them is a cleanup. The design docs are being brought in line (DESIGN.md is another lane's).
7. The Runescape invitation's ten-minute Truthcoin freeze and grants are **built in wave 5** (A9; the invitation is out, so C24 stands as printed); trades bypass the approver by design; the approver's `every 3` counts every send globally (`LOOM_RL_WALLET` 30/min is the only brake); since wave 5 an offer resolves the entry by id, folded id or title (A1), and a buy of an entry that doesn't exist is refused (409 "they don't have that to sell") before any card is minted — a buy never checks what the seller holds (that would let anyone probe another guest's codex), so a buy of an entry the seller lacks fails at Accept and refunds; refused offers count against `LOOM_RL_WALLET`; a held transfer approves itself after 5 min (SW-WALLET-02, fixed: server half, A2).
8. `restrict … to` lists places by name even if hidden; **accepted (wave 5, decision 18):** the `# quarantine` escort pin reaches every performer booth + mods, not only the Antivirus (`# quarantine` is a staff back room every booth reads; the pin has no `!`, so it never chimes, and the Antivirus cue is the Enforcer readout), and a pin dealt during the login cutscene shows inline as an alert on rails and sticks once they press Continue; stale `here` / `needsYou` across restrict / unrestrict unverified on a device.
9. Push semantics changed (K-31a): a `!` alert addressed to one guest or a faction no longer pushes the booths (booth cues ride the Enforcer readouts); `core/server/push.ts`'s header and `play/07-performers` describe the new rule.
10. The King: nothing device-checked (hit feel, the ⚔ / ♟ buttons, glyph fonts on old tablets, grandmaster stalls); hp 8 / one hit per 2 s since wave 5 (≈ 16 s of hits to pawns; grandmaster think time is a device check); the fight is per session; the tablet says when a win didn't land (since wave 6 a reload after that notice posts nothing: a host presses ♟; only a send the page died in the middle of is re-posted, within 60 s); since wave 6 a loss, a draw or a pawns-only stalemate deals a new game at the same strength, and only checkmate opens the door; `/api/mod/signal label` is honoured only when the caller's name is the literal "Director" (same rule as impersonate). U17: the Lichess phone + ♟ stay the fallback.
11. Engine: a character's own card hook must be `when <kind> answered for who:`; `show … with question: P1` must be quoted. Since wave 5 (B1) a comma inside a quoted `with` value is safe, and loomcheck warns on an unquoted `question:`, a bare `when <kind> answered:` in a class and unbalanced quotes in a `fire` / `show`.
12. *Closed in wave 5:* an unparsed `currency:` / `wallet:` line is an editor + loomcheck error (L1203 / L1204, B2), and a clean stop (SIGTERM / SIGINT, `dispose`) flushes the story clock's idle ticks (A7); a hard kill can still lag it by ≤ 15 s.
13. `FactsFiling.at` is "first seen by this server" (after a restart the Judging card's pending order falls back to ledger order); `/api/prime/widget` is not judged; a generic runtime test dealing a `fact` card must pass `factsFile`.
14. *Closed in wave 5 (E1, E3):* the editor's performer lens lists the booth's pinned rooms (Quest Log / Codex / Inbox / Wallet), the operator lens renders wallet rows as cards, and the Inspector has a Restricted field with Restrict / Unrestrict (a bare `restrict`: only the story's `restricted` / `unrestricted` rules run, not its mark — fire `mark_for_reinstallation` / 🩹 for that). Still open: the booth SSE `history` carries pinned-room rows (unmeasured on a long run).
15. The Rust engine and the TextMate grammar do not know `restrict` / `pin` / `unpin` / `currency:` / `wallet:`; since wave 5 the bank compiler delegates `restrict` / `unrestrict` / `pin` / `unpin` / the `quest_*` verbs to the host and warns that a generator's `while` is live-engine only (B16) (cosmetic; no banked example uses them). `scripts/ops/predeploy.sh` runs `titi:facts --check`; the wave-4 helper is `scripts/accept/wave4-wallet.sh` (the wallet, C·5); the King's tent-tablet path is step 5 of `scripts/accept/K-27.md`.

### Wave 5 status (built 2026-10-16, committed as 85d2bad, not deployed)

Wave 5 is the stock-take of every item still open after wave 4: each open issue, each promise an invitation already made, and each wave 2–4 follow-up, sorted into build now / accept / defer (`scratchpad/plan.md`). Six lanes built it: **A** core server · **B** core engine + the show story (one owner for every show file) · **D** play · **E** editor · **F** terminal + screen state · **G** stagehand + reinstall / house. Four integration units (**X1–X4**) took the hand-offs between lanes. Each lane then had a review pass, and its fixes are in. It was committed as **85d2bad** ("wave 5", on top of 9d35a4b; **a35f175** added `ship.sh`'s VPS git record) and is **not deployed**. **Suites at 85d2bad** (re-run as the final gate after the open-issue fixes, every gate green): core 1257 incl. the Postgres-gated tests (74 files); play 196; editor 269; terminal 175; reinstall/web 305; house 152 + 4 skipped; bank 145; stagehand 263; ops scripts 103 (`scripts/ops/test/run.sh`); accept live-guard + burst 10; reinstall/vr 255 + `quest.sh` 26 (autopilot reached all 12 show duck spots); `tsc` clean in core, editor and bank; the terminal, reinstall and play builds clean; `titi:facts --check` "5 outputs up to date"; loomcheck `--smoke` 31 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings (147 events, no pending choices). `pnpm house:sync` regenerated 27 goldens with no change. **The show's model:** 25 quests, 110 INTERACTIONs.

**Deploy:** see the Deploy log above (2026-10-16, wave 5). Two new `.env` knobs, both off by default: `LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS` (K-32's contingency) and `LOOM_HOMESCREEN` (K-31b).

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (2026-10-16, applied, not re-asked):**
1. Runescape: her invitation is out and promises grants and a ten-minute freeze → built (A9, option B); C24 stands as printed.
2. DraftKings: a majority of the coin via `sum()`, 1000 kept as a progress step, a floor of five other cleared programs.
3. Truth Pong: a cup unlocks all of that program's round-2 filings; a miss or a mark isn't credited; hosts press 🏓 for every sink.
4. M19: `play_on_the_screens` (text cards on the wall terminals, never the TV, never story slides); "videos" dropped from M19.
5. M20 / Bathroom: marked programs keep 🚻 and Back; Trabolta can't snoop into Disk Cleanup or Bathroom Mode; 🛡 and the marks stay unguarded; SAFE-PLACE says "they wait outside".
6. MS Paint: her invitation promises a painting every guest signs → built (🖌 tally, the quest, G10); the painting itself is physical.
7. Jeeves: wallet sales count toward ten findings; shares to Trabolta don't.
8. Snapshot coalescing ships off (`LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS` default 0).
9. K-31b built now behind `LOOM_HOMESCREEN` (default off): the guest nonce single-use, 12 h, cleared on restart; booths get the instructions only; the "keep Trapped open" iPhone card ships either way.
10. N-04a prebuilt, inert without an mp4.

### Wave 6 status (built 2026-10-17 on branches `oct31-w6/*`, merged to `master`, not deployed)

Four live test runs on scratch servers (door, wallet, hunt, screens) found 25 bugs, 23 of them distinct (the booth-alert and Trust Chart bugs were each reported twice); everything else the testers drove worked end to end. Wave 6 fixed those first, then only the open items that are cheap and make a promise from the story or the invitations true. Eight lanes, one worktree and branch each (`oct31-w6/<lane>`), one logical change per commit with its test, each lane with a review-fix round, merged `--no-ff` in this order: **server** `144b301` → **play** `05a61b3` → **story** `84e303f` → **content** `1160d8b` → **screens** `422a546` → **reinstall** `8db59c7` → **stagehand** `2ac42e0` → **ops-docs** (this lane: the morning-of checklist, the runbook drift, DESIGN, this status). No merge conflicted and none needed a fallout commit. **Not deployed: the VPS still runs `w3-1015`, so the first release with wave 6 ships waves 4, 5 and 6 together** (Deploy log, 2026-10-17).

**Suites on `master` after the last merge:** core 1295 incl. the Postgres-gated tests (74 files; the show test `trapped-in-the-internet` 179); play 215; editor 270 (incl. the help-content checks); terminal 191; reinstall/web 330; house 152 + 4 skipped; stagehand 267; reinstall/vr 255 + `quest.sh` 26; ops scripts 114 (`scripts/ops/test/run.sh`, after ops-docs); bank 145 (unchanged, not re-run); `tsc` clean in core, play, terminal and reinstall; the play, terminal and reinstall builds clean; `titi:facts --check` "5 outputs up to date"; loomcheck `--smoke` 31 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings. **Counts pinned by the show test:** codex 178 entries / 90 codes (+ *The Truther Download Unredacted*), quests 25.

**Bug key:** B1 booths banner a guest's `!` alerts · B2 CAPTCHA stuck after a restart · B3 a director's Release fires `escape` · B4 a restricted program told "not in this room" · B5 booth readouts survive a restart · B6 two "open now" notices at clearance · B7 the wallet ⏳ glyph / a phantom pending · B8 wallet card updates can't be opened · B9 "settles in N s" after a deny · B10 a 429 blocks cancel / decline · B11 a corrected verdict stays a lie · B12 the Trust Chart call-outs never render · B13 the consensus reads a typed alias · B14 G10's painting given to anyone · B15 DraftKings' majority ignores escrow · B16 "1 programs" · B17 the golden trail through the wall after the King falls · B18 the map page shows a stale variant as live · B19 a terminal's `character=trabolta` makes a phantom DM · B20 a failed `chess won` re-posts on reload · B21 a held slide covers the typed-name fallback · B22 the TV mini-map under the VOICE button (B23 = a duplicate of B1 / B12).

1. **CRA approver:** stays global `every 3`; offers stay outside the approver. C16 and the cast pack say "every third transaction". Selling a codex entry copies it (the seller keeps it, U7).

5. **Truther Download:** Sandy redacted until the story names her; the verbatim text is kept in DESIGN §2.12. (The story lane gated the correction on the overshare *on a scenario-1 night* — the only overshare that names her — else the recount / truth.txt: confirm.)
6. **King stalemate or loss:** a new game; only checkmate opens the door.
7. **Featured objectives:** the Featured Briefing unlocks the program's own two C facts; no round-1 quest; the invitation Q&A stays.
8. **Per-character goals at the end:** in Clippy's Purgatory summary only.
9. **The most-secrets prize:** the award copy says "most codex entries"; no quiz.
10. **The Truthers** are The Way Out (text only).
11. **MS Paint — overridden by the user: her invitation is canon** (four freshman years at NSCAD / Yale / The New School / the Beaux-Arts, the refused thesis *MS Paint Is The Most Pure Art Tool…*): the NSCAD letter changed to agree; C09 / C10 stay. Still for the user: the printed Bingo square "Was expelled from art school" (MS Paint's pack tells her to say yes).
12. **Antivirus personnel codes:** the plan is dropped from DESIGN and the README (Bugdom's hunt is the four red Antivirus facts).
13. **Password Manager:** she flags messages in `# hosts` and the director hides them; no booth tool, no passcode.
14. **Still the user's call:** `Trabolta.awake` after an ending (after K-04), and Trabolta's avatar (when the art exists).

Lane calls on top: the orchestrator default is 20480, not the plan's 16384 (a late night measures ≈ 16–17k tokens); the H1–H6 / F1 rewrites stay inert proposals until signed off (applying them crosses four lanes); the King's banner also says DRAW. AGAIN. for a non-stalemate draw.

**Not building before the freeze (10-27 18:00), and why:**
- Wallet countdowns during a pause, the announcement restart gap, the booth history cap, the facts-judge loose ends: pauses and restarts are rare on the night and each window is about a second; the history cap is measured at K-32 first.
- Lookup gating (Trabolta keeps `lookup: false`) and the trust-points disclosure rule: both risk leaking secrets through the agent; the codex line stays vague.
- Slide, ad and award PNGs, and pre-rendered voice: the text cards work; pre-rendered voice only if K-02 (10-20) finds tablets with no voice.
- Light rules for the 5 new rooms: blocked until the bulbs and groups exist.
- Terminal media-ready + remote reload, the takeover on booths, the Password Manager tool, Print sheet extras, recap line counts + bathroom minutes, award-suggest for G10, the S3 guest scanner: each optional or covered by radios or the director; S3 only if the 10-25 run shows native-camera QRs opening in the wrong browser.
- Revoking the Home Screen nonce on Leave: only matters with `LOOM_HOMESCREEN` on, and it stays off.
- The end-of-night secrets quiz, a Truthers channel, the King Thorax nod, VLC / Pong / host invitations: new content for little gain; the copy is reworded instead.
- Every wave 5 deferred item (scoped device tokens, director push + `notify`, the real wager, CeremonyHub, the LAN audio page, per-cable patch bay, the `alexa` rename, the Rust / bank / TextMate parity, the local-folder judge + wallet, a journaled `chose`, per-guest `while:`, the quest subject filter, pointer lock, the flow e2e, loomcheck in CI): shared hubs, hardware, the live config, or post-party cleanup.
- Per-sender `every 3` counting and offers through the approver: decision 1 keeps them.
- **The surveyed map** (`oct31-w6/map`, K-28): blocked on the survey — enter it in `/reinstall/map`, Save to project, re-place the King + guarded door in both variants, goldens + `house:sync`, `test:vr` on both, rebuild the terminal + reinstall bundles, then `sync-show --replace-maps`.

**Deploy order:** the Deploy log above (2026-10-17, wave 6): `predeploy.sh` → `ship.sh w6-10xx` (waves 4–6 together) → on the VPS the server image first → `sync-show` (with `facts.json` and the king maps; `--replace-maps` once) right away, which restarts the show's event, then restart any other non-ended event → `docker compose rm -sf kokoro` + any stale `spatial-house.json` → verify with the mod codes and scripted drivers, never the user's login → the morning-of checklist once.

7. Everything in *Not building* above, and wave 4 / 5's standing items (the King not device-checked, `FactsFiling.at`, the booth history size, the Rust engine + TextMate grammar).

### Wave 7 status (built 2026-10-17 on branches `oct31-w7/*`, merged to `master`, not deployed)

The user's asks: the server-room door gets a **standalone electronic keypad lock** (its code set by hand on the lock, never networked: nothing in software can lock or unlock it); the sensor is **two loops of wire / copper tape that touch when the door opens**; **the code is a clean entry** (the Cold Lock's 90 s disarm window is gone, wrong patch-bay combinations still alarm); guests get the code by **winning at chess** (the King on the tent tablet, or ♟ for the chess phone) or by **a lore riddle**, and the winning majority gets it too; the door opening plays **a cutscene in the server room**, and guests can **talk to Trabolta candidly on a tablet in the server room, "like the Master in Fallout 1"**; plus wave 6's open issues 2–6. Five lanes, one worktree and branch each (`oct31-w7/<lane>`), each with a review-fix round, merged `--no-ff` in this order: **platform** `e617490` → **story** `2b3a23e` → **agent** `41cbf89` → **hardware** `be242eb` → **terminal** `6eb9864`; then **integration** (`oct31-w7/integration`: the lanes' four small fixes 3f6b080, 508bf19, c2f075c, a6c4327, then the **review fixes** below) and **docs** (this lane, `oct31-w7/docs`), both merged after the wave 7 review. **Not deployed: the first release ships waves 4–7 together** (Deploy log, 2026-10-17, wave 7).

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (2026-10-17; veto any):**
1. **The code is the year Trabolta met Sandy** (The Sandy File already says when), not a hunt answer. It lives in **one place**, `facts.json` `door.code` (generated into `Kernel.door_code` and the secret *The Door Code*); never in `Night.*`, never in a Trabolta line, never in a doc or on the radio. Changing it = one `facts.json` edit + `titi:facts` + `sync-show` + reprogram the keypad by hand.
2. **The riddle:** "FOUR DIGITS. THE DOOR REMEMBERS THE YEAR HE MET HER." — on the printed keypad sign and as *The Cold Door* (secret), every cleared program's at the final round (replacing *The Cold Lock*). Small year asides in the lore: a two-digit year under the heart on Page 999 and the ferry papers' 1997 (guests' papers only: the review took the year out of the LiveJournal and The Sandy File, which Trabolta holds, ccbaf42). The recount's "every day since 1978" now says "since the day I met her".
3. **Who gets the code outright:** a checkmate **from the Y2K hour** (`Night.step >= 14`), once (a duplicate only notes `# hosts`; before step 14 practice); `chess_won` became a subject rule, so the cue sheet and the director's phone ask who won; the majority's Whole Truth gives it to every member.
4. **The cutscene latch:** The Heart plays once, from step 14, before any ending; another opening within 120 s of the last does nothing; a later one plays a short *Back To The Heart*; before step 14 only a `# hosts` note. `🔄 reset` re-arms it.
5. **The second wrong combination alarms** (everyone is forgiven one); whoever is at the cables goes to the Internet. The door never alarms.
6. **Talking him down is human-confirmed:** `stand_down` only offers (proof: *What Sandy Says* or *The Sandy File*, checked by the story); 🕊 `talked_down` ends the night the Combo A way. 🕊 with no offer still works (the director's call).
7. **The secrecy fix:** The Legendary Fact, the combo halves, the riddle and the code are `secret: true` (the orchestrator used to read the first five as ground truth).
8. **Trabolta never says the year he met her** (agent lane, beyond the spec): it is the code. Since the review no entry he holds carries it, and the worker's `secrets.never` (the code, '97 and every combo pair, generated by `titi:facts`) keeps one a program types out of his memory and his voice.
9. **A room terminal fails closed:** an unknown or misspelt room keeps it asleep all night ("the story has no room called …").

Verify with the mod codes and scripted drivers (`scripts/accept/K-27.ts`, never a live event), never the user's login, then the morning-of checklist (§11, item 19).

### Wave 8 status (built 2026-10-17 on branches `oct31-w8/*`, merged to `master`, shipped as `w8-1018`)

Every committed item was built by wave 7, so wave 8 went looking for what breaks, the way wave 6 did. **Find:** nine testers drove the show over HTTP (and headless Chromium at phone size) on their own scratch servers — the whole night doors → ceremony (150 checks), every ending + the ceremony, crash / restart recovery (SIGTERM and SIGKILL mid-burst, deep diffs of every view), the nine performer booths, the phone UI, the screens through the night, the editor's Run mode, Trabolta's pipeline and secrecy (a fake worker and the real stagehand worker on a stub model), and load (K-32's mix, a door rush, 26 minutes of steady traffic); a stock-take re-checked wave 7's open issues and the not-building list, and a docs pass checked every crew card, help article and runbook against the code. Independent verifiers tried to refute each finding: **72 of 83 stood** (1 blocker, 11 major). **Build:** six lanes, one worktree and branch each, each with an independent review and a fix round, merged `--no-ff` in this order: **server-a** `c3b629e` → **server-b** `31f2009` → **story** `b4cf6f3` → **play** `5195260` → **editor** `98ca470` → **edges** `138e500` (no conflicts); then **integration** `6f414be` (the fallout where the story's cue flags met the cue-sheet engine, a recheck that re-ran every tester's driver on the merged tree, and a review of the integration commits) and **docs-show** `fe03135` + **docs-help** `944699a`. **Shipped as `w8-1018` and checked live** (Deploy log, 2026-10-18).

**Suites on `master` after the last merge:** core 1386 incl. the Postgres-gated tests (77 files; the show test 210); play 240; editor 290 (incl. the help-content checks); terminal 211; reinstall/web 338; house 152 + 4 skipped; bank 145; stagehand 302; ops scripts 118; `tsc` clean in core, play, editor, terminal and reinstall; the play, terminal and reinstall builds clean; `titi:facts --check` "5 outputs up to date"; loomcheck `--smoke` 31 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings. **Measured:** a 40-guest, 4,252-line journal restores in ≈ 4–7 s (was 34–101 s); a tick at 60 guests 1.3 ms (was 11.7); a stalled director console holds ≤ 2.6 MB (was 139 MB in 3 min, growing until the process died).

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (2026-10-17; veto any):**
1. **Once truth.txt is read, the server room can't end the night** (T2-03): from 📄 the patch bay, 🕊 and The Heart only post a `# hosts` note.
2. **⚖ is its own cue** (T6-01): 📄 holds the truth.txt slide while someone reads it aloud; ⚖ plays the verdict.
3. **A marked program can't pick or change a side** (T4-07; README: "every button is off").
4. **Reconnect history is capped** for phones and booths at 400 public lines, keeping every alert, card, own thread, pinned room and pin (T9-05); director consoles are not capped.
5. **The voice model's context is pinned at 32768** for an Ollama voice (wave 7 issue 3; it is what runs today).
6. **The cue sheet suggests nothing during the Y2K hour** (midnight fires 🌑 itself; a tap would cut the hour), and 🗜 is an anytime cue, not a NEXT.
7. **Clippy's 🪦 stays a step** (in sequence, live only after Combo B), next to 🏍.
8. **The side chooser after an ending** (T5-06, cosmetic) is not built.

**Deploy order:** the Deploy log above (wave 8): `predeploy.sh --vr` → `ship.sh w8-10xx` → the server image → `sync-show` at once (its reload restarts the show's event) → restart any other non-ended event → on the house Mac restart the `agents` + `show` jobs, `stagehand check --only agents` → reprint the cast packs → verify with the mod codes and scripted drivers (the find phase's drivers live in the session scratchpad; `scripts/accept/K-27.ts`), never the user's login, then the morning-of checklist (§11).

**Open issues after wave 8:**
1. **G11's "press again" ask ticks as fired:** the first G11 before the last award only asks in `# hosts`, but it writes a world flag, so the cue sheet marks it LAST and the phone says ✓ Fired. Press again within 30 s, as the card says.
2. Rarer spellings of the code still pass `secrets.never` ("one, nine, nine, seven" with commas between the words, "nineteen 97", "it was 97 when we met"); "in 97%" is withheld. The human 🕊 and `lookup: false` stay.
3. Hardware-fallback cues pressed early (🖨, 🫘, 🔌 / 💥 / ❌) are refused silently (they share a signal with the hardware).
4. A booth's SSE stream carries other booths' lobby readouts (rendered nowhere; wire only).
5. The editor Log's time column on a server run runs the console's wall clock through the story clock.
6. Each full director console is ≈ 6 Mbit/s at 45 guests (the full ModView every frame); K-32's script has no bandwidth pass line.
7. A restart still ends every reinstallation walk (documented: rescan).
8. The side chooser stays up after an ending (T5-06); ceremony and award slides are text cards with no winner (intended).
9. **Nothing is on a device yet:** every fix is unit-, runtime- or scratch-server-tested; the device checks are in the table.
10. Everything in wave 7's open list not closed above (the device checks, the user's C16 invitation wording), wave 6's *Not building* list and wave 4 / 5's standing items.

### Wave 9 status (built 2026-10-18 on branches `oct31-w9/*`, merged to `master`, shipped as `w9-1018`)

Wave 8 shipped, so wave 9 went looking again, wave 8's way. **Find:** fourteen testers drove the shipped show (`w8-1018`) on their own scratch servers — the wallet end to end, the hunt + the judge + the bottle, the reinstallation games + the King, the control plane on Postgres (boot, restart, sync-show), the crew cards followed literally, an adversarial guest with only the public codes, a whole night in real browsers, the stagehand ↔ story contract, every timer across pause / resume / restart, the nine featured sheets, regression reviews of wave 8's server and client diffs, a stock-take of the known open items, and ops + deploy — 3,441 checks in all. Independent verifiers tried to refute each finding: **all 53 stood** (the two reported blockers were judged major: 19 major, 28 minor, 6 cosmetic). **Build:** nine lanes, one worktree and branch each, each with an independent review and a fix round, merged `--no-ff` in this order: **server-a** `8b47141` → **server-b** `b554630` → **console-delta** `5a5cfc8` → **story-cues** `15f9dbd` → **story-rules** `20a07f4` → **play** `599a95c` → **editor** `d9f0ca7` → **edges** `ea2ca23` → **ops** `ed9fe38`; then **integration** `0797b1e` (the cross-lane seams, a review, and a recheck that re-ran every tester's driver on the merged tree: 46 fixed, 5 partly, 2 not in scope, 16 new problems) and **integration-2** `ae79117` (the recheck's new problems). 162 commits from `1aeecd7`. **Not deployed:** it ships as `w9-1018` (rollback `w8-1018`; Deploy log).

**Late decision (2026-10-18): the agent hub's stall handling is parked.** Integration-2 built a hold line + hand-over for a worker that stays connected but stops answering (6ec3b15 … 00b0810). Three review rounds each found new major bugs, most in the hand-over paths between workers, which the show never runs (one laptop voices Trabolta). It was reverted before shipping (`74501d1`, plus `c0decad` for Help 43) and is parked on `oct31-w9/agent-stall`. The hub ships as the server-a lane and the first integration round left it (open issue 1).

**Suites on `master` (ae79117), re-run for this docs pass:** core 1515 incl. the Postgres-gated tests (86 files; the show test 210, `trapped-w9-cues` 16, `trapped-w9-rules` 31); play 261; editor 309; terminal 212; reinstall/web 356; house 152 + 4 skipped; bank 145; stagehand 308; ops scripts 210; `tsc --noEmit` clean in core; `titi:facts --check` "5 outputs up to date"; loomcheck `--smoke` 31 files, 0 errors / 0 warnings. VR 259 headless checks + `quest.sh` 26 (the edges lane; no GDScript changed after it). **Measured:** a full director console fell from 5.86 to 0.12 Mbit/s at 45 guests (K-32: 45 guests, 9 booths, 6 terminals, 2 directors, PASS); a 5.7 MB history reaches a console on a 120 KB/s link without a drop; 60 guests DMing Trabolta at 6 s a reply: 60/60 answered, 0 cancels.

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (2026-10-18; veto any):**
1. **D1 McAfee holds no pots and takes no coin cut** (a performer has no Wallet): every story text says a bet settles player to player (the winner sends a 🙏 Request, the loser pays) and McAfee referees in `# truthcoin-exchange`; 🪙 Pay / Charge stay corrections.
2. **D2 💡 Restore the house lights cancels a pending automatic dance** (`Night.dance_at`) and says so in `# hosts`; it does not lift the takeover (🪩 does).
3. **D3 the bottle picks any cleared, free program not in the Internet**, not only those whose last check-in was the Desktop.
4. **D4 a ✍ admin toggle lets the second director's own award lines replace the template lines** (Trabolta's host branches only; Clippy, the sounds and the slides unchanged).
5. **D5 the Judging card gets override buttons on every judged filing** (both modes); a booth's 📝 press with several open filings refuses with a `# hosts` line pointing at the Judging card.
6. **D6 sync-show / sync-example refuse to reload a live event that has real guests** unless `--i-know-this-is-live` (files are still written).
7. **D7 a guest can only join / defect to a declared, joinable, revealed faction** (403 otherwise).
8. **D8 guest passes render their QR in the browser** (no `/api/qr`).
9. **D9 a right guest event code always passes the per-IP lockout.**
10. **D10 a paused event commits an agent's words but refuses its acts** ("doors are closed", not counted against a limit), and the hub's timers skip the pause.
11. *(integration, e9f0c0f)* **A printer lid opened late keeps the night's look**: after 🤖 or in the Y2K hour it plays only the beans line, the spill and −20 composure (revert e9f0c0f if a late lid should still take the glitch look).
12. *(2026-10-18)* **The stall handling ships parked** (above).

**Not building** (as the build script said): T8-stagehand-04 (the kitchen restore's brightness), wave 8 open issues 2–4. T5-crew-cards-06 was a docs fix (the Director card's 🔄 row now says press 💡 if the house is still red), done in this pass.

**Deploy order:** the Deploy log above (wave 9): `predeploy.sh --vr` → `ship.sh w9-1018` → the server + proxy images (`release.sh` waits on `/api/health`; `deploy/release.sh --check-env` ends ✔) → `sync-show` at once, no `--replace-maps` (its reload restarts the show's preview event on the wave 9 story) → restart any other non-ended event from Run → on the house Mac restart the `show` job → reload every tablet and the TV → re-export the Quest APK if the Quest is go → reprint the cast packs → verify with the mod codes and scripted drivers (`scripts/accept/K-24.ts setup`, `K-27.ts setup`), never the user's login, then the morning-of checklist (§11).

**Open issues after wave 9:**
1. **A wedged but connected laptop delays the hold line** (the parked stall handling): a worker that stays on the stream but stops answering keeps each DM waiting out its own 2-minute reply clock in turn, so a burst can wait many minutes with "typing…" and no hold line. Directors still answer from Run (be Trabolta in the thread); the tech op restarts the `agents` job. The work is on `oct31-w9/agent-stall` for a deliberate redesign.
2. **ALL STOP doesn't pause the run** (T9-timers-01, the procedure half): 💡 holds the dance, but other story timers (midnight's final-reveal look, the clock warnings) still fire during an ALL STOP that isn't paused. Whether ALL STOP gains a "⏸ Pause the run" step is the user's call (OP-20, the emergency card).
3. **Common phrasings of a right answer are still judged confident lies** until the team adds aliases to `facts.json` (T2-facts-01, the content half; the slider starts at 100). The Judging card's per-filing buttons are the fix on the night.
4. The kitchen restore after a Start Bar cut still goes to full brightness in a dim look (T8-stagehand-04, not built); `Night.lit_scene` now stops the catch-up from replaying it once the look moves on.
5. A booth's view still carries the `who: admin` cues (≈ 12 of 34 KB per booth frame; console-delta's optional half, not built).
6. After a long pause the tablet's "Time in the simulation" counts the pause; the recap's speedrun doesn't (cosmetic).
7. An editor reopening the collab stream after a server restart rebases its text onto the server's doc: a co-writer's edit to the same span within one back-off step (≤ 15 s) can be overwritten.
8. Wave 8 open issues 2–4 stand (rarer spellings of the door code pass `secrets.never`; the hardware fallbacks pressed early are refused silently; a booth's SSE stream carries other booths' lobby readouts), and 7–8 (a restart ends every reinstallation walk; the side chooser after an ending). Wave 8's 1, 5 and 6 are closed above.
9. **Nothing is on a device yet:** every fix is unit-, runtime- or scratch-server-tested; the device checks are in the table.
10. Everything in wave 8's open list not closed above, wave 7's device checks, wave 6's *Not building* list and wave 4 / 5's standing items.

### Wave 10 status (built 2026-10-18 on branches `oct31-w10/director-*`, merged as 3eac8de, shipped as `w10-1018`)

Asked for: a Director page a director can call the night from without hunting. Built from one spec in four lanes, each in its own worktree, merging `--no-ff` in this order: **core → show → editor → phone**, then an integration branch and a docs branch. Display only: `refusedPress`, `notePress`, NEXT / LAST, the journal's format and the bank are unchanged (a core test walks the show through 30 presses with and without the `act:` lines and compares every mark). Not display only: `cueState` carries `presses` / `pressed` (a per-log press log, rebuilt on replay), and `/api/mod/signal` turns down a cue-sheet press with 409 `moved` (`pressMovedArm`) or 409 `doorsClosed` (a paused run whose `start:` has not played), its 200 carrying `presses`.

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (veto any):** acts are an INTERACTION key, not a declaration (no new keyword 13 days out); ⏳ and 🚨 move to C, 🕚 opens E and 🏍 / 🪦 close F in the README's tables (docs only); 💡 is Anytime so a safety cue never sits in a folded act; the Kernel's door code, combos, majority and lies show on the laptop masked (press and hold 👁 to read one for 5 s) and never reach the phone; a stale arm is dropped, never re-armed; on the laptop, after NEXT itself is pressed, the new NEXT refuses a click for 1 s (an aside or any other press starts no cooldown); on the phone every press — an aside too — starts that 1 s on NEXT.

**Open issues:** the editor's who? picker sorts by name only (the view does not say which room a cue belongs to); the phone's cue-code chip loses its outline on a muted fired row (cosmetic); nothing is on a device yet.

### Wave 11 status (built 2026-10-18 on branches `oct31-w11/*`, merged to `master` as 1a67497, shipped as `w11-1019`)

The user's design of 2026-10-18, in their words: Trabolta's **heart** lives in the server room (cold storage) behind the real keypad, and there are two ways to the code, **QR clues (one clue = one digit)** and **beating the King at chess**. *The chess game is only on the terminal outside the server-room door*, in real life, with **no hints**, awake all night, and a win gives the code all night. The King also stands in **both reinstallation games** with **one shared health bar for all guests, 15 HP for the whole night**; each guest may hit him **once per reinstallation visit**; the bar sets how well he plays the real chess (full = grandmaster … 0 = pawns only), and at 0 **he kneels in the simulation for everyone**. Opening the door (the copper-wire contact → `server_door_opened`) plays **The Heart**, however the door was unlocked: like the Master in Fallout 1, getting in doesn't let you do anything, but Trabolta becomes honest and open, and guests can scan their pass and **give him codex files** at the heart terminal. Four secret **digit posters**; all four auto-assemble *The Door Code* and the quest *The Cold Door*; **the riddle is no longer a path**; the majority path stays. **Guest avatars in the reinstallation games use their real profile pictures.** And three of the workaround bundles offered that day: **guest-facing** (an in-app 📷 Scan + the wrong-browser landing, a guest countdown strip, the featured programs reserved with claim links), **judging + majority** (near-miss judging + an unset slider, a present-only Y2K majority) and **crew load** (booth arm-then-fire, the performer copies of 🔌 💥 ❌ 🚪 🫘 + 🔧 `crew_through_the_door`, the self-serve Windows Defender door part, Trabolta's busy line + single-worker stall detection). **Not chosen, not built:** ALL STOP as a run pause, house health, stagehand store-and-forward.

Built from one spec in nine lanes, each in its own worktree with a review and a fix round, merged `--no-ff` in this order: **spatial** `73c41f9` → **server-a** `7130fdc` → **server-b** `bca897e` → **story** `d3eeeb1` → (`aede35d`: loomcheck knows `busy:`) → **games** `50513b0` → **chess** `05b2ea0` → **vr** `28a025a` → **play** `c7facac` → **editor** `f476df6` → `92c745f` (a spatial test follows the show's Power On arming). Then **integration** (`oct31-w11/integration`, 16 commits to `5513dd3`): the show wired to the lanes' new keys, `by:` enforced on the booths, and an intent review's findings 1–6 fixed; then a **final fix pass** on the same branch (13 commits to `f2f2e3f`, merged to master as `1a67497`), then `oct31-w11/docs-{help,show}` and `oct31-w11/docs-final`. **Shipped as `w11-1019`** on 2026-10-19 (rollback `w10-1018`; the Deploy log).

**Suites on master after the final fix pass:** core 1780 incl. the Postgres-gated tests (95 files; loomcheck `--smoke` 0 / 0; `titi:facts --check` up to date); house 179 + 4 skipped; reinstall/web 327; terminal 245; play 321; editor 398; chess 23; bank 145; stagehand 310; VR 401 headless checks + `quest.sh` 26. (On `oct31-w11/integration` at 5513dd3: core 1742, play 318, VR 399 at `92c745f`.) Live on a no-DB scratch server: a hit took the bar to 14, then end → begin → hit answered 409 `already hit` three times and the bar stayed 14; a digit note shared to Trabolta answered 403 *they won't take a secret file*; Norton's 🔧 answered 200, Clippy's 403 *not your button … is for Antivirus*.
