**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (2026-10-18; veto any):**
1. **D1 The Heart needs Trabolta awake** (`introduce_trabolta`; he stays strictly offline before it, the user's 2026-10-14 call). Before it, inside a 🔧 crew window and after 📄 truth.txt an opening only tells `# hosts`. The patch bay, `stand_down` and 🕊 stay the Y2K hour's (`Night.step >= 14`): before then Trabolta is candid at his heart, but nothing in the room ends the night.
2. **D2 a reinstallation visit is a walk to the duck** (widened in the integration review): a hit stays spent across Leave, a director's end, a timeout and any re-scan until that guest finds a duck; a find ends the visit; a story reset forgets every visit. The record is in memory, so a server restart lets each guest hit once more. The alternative is a time-based cooldown.
3. **D3 the bar is a story world var** (`Night.king_hp`, journaled, restart-safe); only the server lowers it, on a validated hit; 👑 and Run → House map's Set… raise it.
4. **D4 a ghost's picture is a Win98 tile head** over the capsule plus the title-bar tag's icon; no picture = today's tinted capsule.
5. **D5 a marked program can't challenge the King** (*⛔ Marked programs can't challenge the King.*); one game per pass scan; scan again to play again.
6. **The patch-bay buttons are the four room Antivirus' booths'** (`by: Norton Anti-Virus, McAfee Anti-Virus, Password Manager, CRA Agent Software`, story rules; the final fix pass took Windows Defender off, the door-only part), since the read-through hasn't picked who holds the server room ([[EDIT]] on the patch-bay card); every Host's, performer's and Windows Defender's booth is refused. If the read-through picks CRA only, set `by: CRA Agent Software` and edit the cards.
7. **The chess terminal shows the bar and the stakes line only** (*Checkmate him and the server-room door code goes to your Codex.* / *No prize now.*), never *The King is rattled.* … (the tent tablet's HUD keeps those).
8. **A near miss is proposed accurate**: if no director judges it, the round's settle credits it (it counts toward consensus); the ⚠ line in `# hosts` is the safeguard. Needs the user's call (open issue 1).
9. **Guests see only the countdowns they are told about out loud** (Bingo, hunt 1, the filing, the summons, midnight).
10. **`arm: true` marks a press with no way back**: an ending (🔌 💥 💘), the alarm (❌), The Heart (🚪), two minutes of the room (🔧), a secret handed over (♟ 🍎), a once-a-night moment (🫘), the guest's 💾.
11. **Windows Defender is an Enforcer with no lines** (the door for late arrivals); its scan clears a program like any Antivirus's.
12. *(lanes)* `nearMatch` is word by word and tighter than the spec (1–3 letters exact, a misspelled word keeps its first letter); a reserved program's `wiki:` article is held like the program; the claim is kept in the browser that joined; the editor's 🔓 also shuts a window early; a secret file reads *A SECRET FILE: HE WON'T TAKE IT* on the heart terminal (the play app's Share… leaves Trabolta out for it since the final fix pass).

**Deploy order** (the Deploy log, wave 11): on the Mac `scripts/ops/predeploy.sh --vr` → `scripts/ops/ship.sh w11-10xx`; on the VPS (1) **the server + proxy images first** (the entrypoint's migration adds `event.claim_secret`; no new `.env` key; `deploy/release.sh --check-env` must still end ✔), because the new `validateHouse` is what takes `hp: 15`, `health` and the `hit` / `fallen` signals, so an old server refuses the new maps; (2) **right away `sync-show --replace-maps`** (the maps changed: the King's `hp: 15` + `health` + `armed`, the chess-phone prop gone, the `server-room-door` spot; check first that the project's maps hold no survey edits worth keeping) **with its reload**, which starts the preview fresh on the wave 11 story (Power On seeds the bar; never `--no-reload` this sync); restart any other non-ended event from Run; (3) on the house Mac `git pull` (the persona and the mind file changed) and `uv run stagehand launchd restart --job agents` (the `show` job and `trapped.house.yaml` did not change); (4) reload every wall terminal, the TV and the tent tablet; provision the Server Door tablet `…/terminal/?code=<mod>&name=Server Door&spot=server-room-door&view=chess` and retire the chess phone; (5) re-export the Quest APK if the Quest is go; (6) print the invitations from Run → Print sheet (one claim QR per featured program), the four silver digit notes and the new keypad sign, and reprint the cast packs; (7) verify with the mod codes and scripted drivers, never the user's login. **A rollback to `w10-1018`** must also sync the wave 10 story and maps back (`--replace-maps`): an old `validateHouse` refuses `hp: 15`.

**Open issues after wave 11:**
1. **Answered by the user (2026-10-19), built in wave 11b:** *"Can't Trabolta judge things?"* Trabolta now rules on every near miss when his worker is online (§5 *Wave 11b status*); the credit at the settle below stands only for a near miss filed with no worker online, or one he never answered. Was: **A near miss nobody judges is credited** at the round's settle (`facts.ts` returns accurate with `near`), and that includes real but different answers that count as near (*Groq* or *Gronk* for Grok, *Mrs Pickles* for Mister Pickles) and, since the final fix pass, a right answer said at length (*11 years ago* for 11). An abbreviation (*Mr. Pickles*, *0900*) is simply accurate, with no ⚠. The user's call: hold an unjudged near miss with no credit and no mark (a new state in the story's settle), or print the ⚠ check on the director's card before 📋 `read_the_results`. Until then the director checks every ⚠ line before 📋.
2. **D2's visit** can be vetoed for a cooldown; a server restart mid-show lets each guest hit once more.
3. ~~The play app's Codex *Share…* still offers secret entries to Trabolta~~ Closed in the final fix pass: *Share…* leaves him out for a secret entry or one about someone or somewhere else (the server's own refusal).
4. `by:` is not enforced in the editor's local-folder rehearsal (the in-browser Sim), so another booth's patch-bay press lands there; on a server project it is refused.
5. A stalled worker whose stagehand dropped its cancelled task stays stalled until it reconnects (`stagehand launchd restart --job agents`); directors answer from Run meanwhile.
6. The editor's 🔓 countdown is the server's time against the laptop's clock (skew moves it until the next snapshot); the play app's check-in landing names a room from its slug (CPU / RAM / TV kept upper-case).
7. The read-through still picks which of the four room Antivirus holds the server room (patch-bay card, CRA's card); Windows Defender never does.
8. **Nothing is on a device yet**: the King's hit feel on the tablet and in the headset, the Server Door terminal on an old tablet (the 60 kB chess chunk, think time at grandmaster), pictures in a headset, the busy line live, invitations printed and scanned. The device checks are in the table.
9. Wave 10's and wave 9's open issues stand, except wave 9's 1 (the wedged laptop: the stall detection now posts the hold line after 60 s) and its 3 (common phrasings: near-miss judging catches the mistyped ones; aliases still need adding).

### Wave 11b status (built 2026-10-19 on branches `oct31-w11b/*`, merged to `master`, shipped as `w11b-1019`)

The user's asks after the wave 11 live check, in their words: *"do we really need Directors to judge everything? Can't Trabolta judge things?"* (wave 11's open issue 1) and *"marked programs can use the terminal, but Trabolta should change what he says / thinks of them, still letting them continue"*. Built in two lanes, **judge-server** and **worker**, merged `--no-ff` into `oct31-w11b/integration` (9c47c70, e39b4e1), which took the show's last fix and merged to master as 299f38d; then **live-fixes** (58c8be6, from the `w11-1019` live check); then three commits on master (d5e9515, fd901dd, 431d542) and this docs branch.

**Suites on master at 431d542:** core 1800 (98 files, incl. the Postgres-gated tests; `titi:facts --check` up to date), editor 400 (52 files), stagehand 334. No other package changed. Live on `gpt-oss:20b`: five rulings in 18 s; *Mr Pickels*, *Grokk*, *11 years ago*, *9:00 in the morning* and *Mister Pickles the cat* were ruled the same, *Mrs Pickles* and *Groq* not.

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (veto any):**
1. **A ruling is asked once, at the filing.** A near miss filed while no worker is online keeps wave 11's promise (credited at the settle unless a director judges it), because `# hosts` was already told so; it is never asked when a worker connects later. Only a server restore asks the ones it finds still pending.
2. **Same → accurate; not → by the confidence** (a lie at 100, else uncertain), exactly as the facts judge would have called a clear miss.
3. **The director always wins:** a verdict before the ruling cancels it, a verdict after it overrides it (the later verdict wins), and review mode asks nothing.
4. **The ruling is not Trabolta speaking:** the key goes into that one call only, never his voice, his mind, the orchestrator or `/api/agent/facts`, and the call sees no confidence, persona or state.
5. **The mark is public to him; the reason is not:** `marked` is in `them:`, `marked_for` is not.

**Deploy order** (the Deploy log, wave 11b): on the Mac `scripts/ops/predeploy.sh --vr` → `scripts/ops/ship.sh w11b-1019`; on the VPS the server + proxy images (the editor's Judging card ships in the proxy; no migration, no new `.env` key, no map change), then `sync-show` with its reload (**no `--replace-maps`**: the maps did not change) so the story carries `judge: Trabolta` and the moved code; restart any other non-ended event. On the house Mac `git pull` (the persona, the mind file's `secrets.never` and stagehand's judge) and `uv run stagehand launchd restart --job agents`; the `show` job is unchanged. **Program the keypad** to the new `facts.json` `door.code`. A rollback to `w11-1019` needs no story sync: the old server ignores the `judge:` header, so near misses wait for a director as in wave 11. **Never sync the wave 11 story back:** its door code is the old one, the one in the public bio.

1. **Trabolta's reflect budget** is near the orchestrator's 20480-token context: room for a sentence or two more. Raise `num_ctx` in `laptop.yaml` before adding persona or mind text.
2. **The F2 answer (Grok) is in his voice prompt** through the LiveJournal codex entry he holds. A story / secrets call: mark that entry's answer for `secrets.lore`, take it off him, or accept it.
3. **The qwen orchestrator rarely moves per-person trust**, so the mark's effect is mostly in his voice at the heart, not in his dossier numbers.
4. The mind file's MARKED PROGRAMS section still tells the orchestrator that outside his heart the mark is "a passing remark", which fd901dd dropped from the voice. A brief could bring the repeated line back; trim that bullet if it shows up in a rehearsal.
5. The ⚠ line says *asked Trabolta to rule* whenever a worker could rule, but a filing whose same answer was already asked in this server process (a refiling after an expired ruling) is not asked again, so the line can promise a ruling that never comes. The card shows no *asking…* then: judge it yourself.

### Wave 12 status (built 2026-10-19 on branches `oct31-w12/*`, merged to `master` as a954226, shipped as `w12-1019` on 2026-10-20)

The user's asks, in their words: *"we need to radically rethink how Traboltas mind works from a "plumbing" perspective; it seems to me that we are doing a lot of un-needed work in the process. there are a lot of repeated messages when there doesn't need to be. Trabolta should *feel* alive, even if he strictly isn't."* and *"Trabolta shouldn't be weary to any of the actual facts he's truly asking for."* The second was read both ways: he must not be **aware** of the answers (the answers follow-up, first) and must not be **wary** of the facts he asks for (the redesign). The design measured the October rehearsal on the house Mac (event d47c250c): about 23 model calls per guest line, 445 surveys for 21 replies (321 after the last guest line, 307 while he was asleep), 88 of 96 brief rewrites overwritten unread, the catchphrase in 19 of 21 replies, the story's receipts fed back as 54–71 % of his own turns, a 184 KB `mind.json`, a reply p50 of 12.3 s. The user then chose **"Build it as designed"** (unload the second model; the quiet-conversation summary stays on only if K-04's p95 stays under 10 s; he does not speak first for Oct 31), **"The house judges it"** for the pickup line and **"Scrub the film from his own files"** for the film.

**What it is now (`mind_mode: new`, the story mind).** One voice call per guest line, no survey, no reflection, the second model never started. Plain code applies the mind file's **phase card** for `Night.phase` the moment it changes (stage, favours, drives, live powers, what he may tell, the brief: one revision *set by story*), builds a **person card** for whoever speaks (first-met line, the voice's own ≤ 140-character memos, what they gave him, what they filed as ids, the mark, the Trust Chart, the story's trust) and orders the prompt so Ollama's cache holds (a one-token warm call first). Code guards every line before it goes out: secrets, hunt answer words the guest never typed, invented facts and hiding places, a name the story has not said, the stock phrase outside first contact, and a line this guest already heard (one retry; in a burst the old sentences are cut). A conversation that goes quiet gets one summary call; a director's **Rewrite brief** is one call. The server tags the story's lines in his threads and adds a short `recent` list; the worker saves and mirrors only on a change and stores the long prompt once. **Classic** (`mind_mode: classic`, also what an absent key means) is the way back, with a survey guard. Help → *The mind debugger*, `stagehand/README.md` → *The story mind*, the crew card `cast-packs/crew/trabolta-mind.md`.

**Built:** the **answers** follow-up on `trabolta/answers` (b64bed2 … f9f421a, the base of every lane), the build contract, eight lanes merged `--no-ff` into `oct31-w12/integration` in the order **S → A0 → H → W1 → W2 → W3 → E → C**, ten integration fixes, the K-04 replay on the real model (with its fixes), two reviews and a fix pass (20 findings, 19 fixed, one a watch item), and this docs branch.

**Suites on `oct31-w12/integration` at 2064550:** core 1847 (100 files, incl. the Postgres-gated suites), editor 432 (52 files) + `tsc -b` clean, play 321, terminal 245, stagehand 711; loomcheck `--smoke` 0 / 0; `titi:facts --check` up to date; `stagehand check --only agents` OK in both minds (the classic late-night reflect estimate is 20478 of its 20480 tokens). Untouched since the verify step and green there: house 179 + 4 skipped, chess 23, bank 145, reinstall/web 327, ops 210.

The first pass of the new mind (4afa616 + cf57e88) failed bar 1 (p95 10576 ms: every line re-read its whole ≈ 4.5 k-token prompt, because gpt-oss's sliding-window cache keeps checkpoints only near a prompt's end; the warm call fixed it) and bar 8 per script (5 of 21 on october-21, 4 of 14 on facts-offers; fixed by scoping the retry to what this guest heard). **The classic column is generous to classic:** at a guest's 30 s pace its qwen reflections were preempted and dropped (0 reflect thoughts in three of its runs), so its real cost was higher; it fails bars 2 and 3 anyway. Over the final runs guard 2b cut 24 invented sentences in 643 replies with no false cut by inspection, and guard 2 (real answer words) never fired. **Watch numbers** (not bars, final): dead-air lines 9 of 132 (6.8 %, over the 3 % watch; 7 of them in burst-30, where nothing is retried); facts-offers' commonest opener 36 % of replies (over 25 %); a right offer drew a question 28 % of the time against 11 % for a wrong one over three runs (over the watch: more runs needed; bar 5 passes).

What he sounds like now (final runs): *"I recall your feline friend Pixel and your nightly shifts at the Lobster Pound."* (a returning guest) · *"Why were you marked? Tell me what you found that made the Antivirus uneasy."* (the marked program at his heart) · *"Thanks for the fact, Napster. Who would you like me to snoop on?"* · *"I do not know why she matters personally, only that her data is useful."* (the hunt hour: *her*, never the name).

**The show text** (lanes C and the fix pass; **the user's sign-off by Thu 10-22**, all in this folder): `trabolta.persona.md` — FACTS ARE FOOD, the programs bring the facts and he never gives one (no answer, guess, count or hiding place), told they will find him something he thanks them; until the house says her name he never says it nor confirms one; a snoop is on another program they name (ask whom first); he does not know which doors are open; at his heart no prices whatever the brief says, and the mark he sees is theirs, not his; the new keys `catchphrases`, `catchphrase_stages: [formal]`, `fallbacks` (two dead-air lines per stage), `unsaid` (Sandy, on the heart situations' own condition). `trabolta.mind.md` — the 27 phase cards and 8 stage cards (each card's `cue` is the old text, lightly edited), the drives without `suspicion`, WHAT HE IS TOLD replaces the judging rules, truth_1's brief buys a snoop on another program they name, the last act's card drops the dialog-box voice, the six `finale_*` briefs end *The hunt is over; nothing more is filed.* `cast/trabolta.loom` — the snoop hook refuses the program he is talking to (words only, nothing spent). `main.loom` — the fact card's line in his thread differs per round. `codex/4-trabolta.loom` — his file no longer quotes the stock phrase.

**Decisions taken on the user's behalf (veto any):**
1. **An absent `mind_mode` is classic**, so a worker restarted before `laptop.yaml` is edited keeps the old mind; the switch is one line.
2. **The quiet summary stays on** (`dossier_on_close: on`): with it on in every run the pooled p95 is 6.5 s, under the user's 10 s rule.
3. **The warm call is on by default** (`voice.warm_head`, `api: ollama` and the lean layout only): a hit saves ≈ 3 s a line, a miss costs about what the re-read it replaces; its time counts in the voice row, so bar 1 is honest.
4. **The repeat retry reads only what this guest heard** (his last 3 lines to them, his replies in the thread, the story's lines there). Lines said to other guests stay a prompt warning (the design's risk 5 option), which leaves some cross-guest sameness.
5. **No retry in a burst:** sentences the guest heard word for word are cut, and nothing left sends a dead-air line (0 exact repeats, at the cost of a few dead-air lines).
6. **The new guards are deterministic** (regex per sentence) and stop at house rooms written *the X*: a lower-case place (*the kitchen*) passes, because a wider rule cut harmless lines.
7. **The share list is the phase card's `tell`**, enforced by the worker only (the server does not know the card); fires are not dropped at `favours: none` (flicker and the screens are not favours; the story's `while:` decides).
8. **The classic prompt is unchanged** (`test_classic_snapshot`), so the way back is the mind he had; the lean wording changes are lean-only.
9. **The new wire has no switch:** debounced saves, change-gated mirrors and stored-once prefixes stay on in classic; only a release tag removes them.
10. **Show-text trims to fit classic's budget:** every persona edit was cut back so the classic late-night reflect fits its 20480 tokens (now 20478), so P1's *FACTS ARE FOOD … while a hunt or a Moment of Truth is open* was not added (the finale briefs carry the hunt-is-over line instead).
11. (docs lane) **Compose now passes `LOOM_AGENT_RECENT`** (9242e3a; blank = on), so rung 7 works on the VPS, and **the help's near-miss examples use an invented key** (51550e3): the play app bundles its articles, so they had named a real answer to any guest reading the JS.

**Deploy order** (the Deploy log's TODO line; wave 12 ships on its own on top of `w11b-1019`, the rollback):
1. Merge to `master` (`--no-ff`), then on the Mac `scripts/ops/predeploy.sh --vr` → `scripts/ops/ship.sh w12-1019`. **The server first:** the wire is additive (an old worker ignores `story`, `recent`, `traceAfter` and `paused`; a new worker against an old server degrades to every thread line being his). No migration, no map change, no new required `.env` key: leave `LOOM_AGENT_RECENT` blank (on). `LOOM_AGENT_RECENT=off` is the server-side switch (a server restart: before doors only).
2. **`sync-show` WITH its reload, before doors** (no `--replace-maps`: no map changed; never `--no-reload`): the persona, the mind file, `cast/trabolta.loom` and `main.loom` changed. Restart any other non-ended event.
3. **On the house Mac** (this Mac's checkout is the house Mac's, so the merge is its pull): in `stagehand/laptop.yaml`, under `agents:`, add `mind_mode: new`. These are the defaults, so they may be left out, but writing them makes each rung one edit:
   ```yaml
   mind_mode: new
   voice:
     memo: on
     repeat_guard: on
     dossier_on_close: on
     warm_head: on        # the voice's llm must be api: ollama (laptop.yaml's is)
     layout: lean
   mind:
     stage_from_phase: on
     phase_poll_s: 30
   ```
   Keep the `orchestrator:` section as it is (the way back). Then `uv run stagehand check --config laptop.yaml --only agents` (*mind: NEW (one voice call per line) …*, *27 phase card(s), 8 stage card(s), 3 catchphrase(s)*) → `uv run stagehand launchd restart --job agents` (the `show` job needs nothing) → **Run → Mind → Reset mind** with the worker connected (rehearsal memos and briefs go) → **unload the second model**: `ollama stop tobestyledintro/qwen3.8-9b-distill:q8_0`; `ollama ps` shows `gpt-oss:20b` alone. Keep the model pulled and `OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=2` for the way back.
4. Reprint the director, second-director and tech-operator cards and the new `cast-packs/crew/trabolta-mind.md`. Nothing on the terminals, the TV, the tablet or the headset changes.

**The kill-switch ladder** (smallest first; rungs 1–6 are one line in `laptop.yaml` + `uv run stagehand launchd restart --job agents`, a few seconds with the hold line covering; the card has the when-to): 1 `voice.memo: off` (no memos, no summaries) · 2 `voice.repeat_guard: off` (one call per line, always) · 3 `voice.dossier_on_close: off` · 4 `mind.stage_from_phase: off` (directors step the stage, as in classic) · 5 `voice.layout: classic` (the old prompt inside the new mind) · 6 `mind_mode: classic` (the whole old mind: qwen loads again, ≈ 25 s at the first reflection; Survey now; the stage by hand) · 7 the server's `LOOM_AGENT_RECENT=off` (before doors) · 8 the release `w11b-1019`, **the house Mac's whole checkout with it** (an older worker refuses the wave 12 persona's `catchphrases:` key, so it must never run against a newer show tree). The show text alone: `git revert` + `sync-show`, before doors. **Rehearse rung 6 and back on 10-25 and time it.**

**Open issues after wave 12:**
1. **Owed device check:** Lane E's slider on the real Mind page against a new-mode worker (the server and worker half is proven: one `set` → one control row and one revision; a same-value `set` → nothing; the editor's grace timer has no browser check).
2. **Recommended show text, the user's call:** hunt_2's brief *you want facts now and you pay for them* drives the remaining invented hints (1–2 lines per 30-line burst: lower-case places, *six facts remaining*, made-up house state); add *they bring them; you have none to give, and you do not know where they are*. And P1's *FACTS ARE FOOD … while a hunt or a Moment of Truth is open* needs room the classic reflect budget doesn't have (trim the classic mind body first).
3. **Watch items from the final runs:** dead air at 6.8 % (most in bursts); facts-offers' replies sound alike (top opener 36 %, *Your fact has been logged.* still appears); the right / wrong question-rate gap (28 % vs 11 %, three runs: needs more); the finale still formal at low effort (*The circuits hum…*, though *logged* / *query* are gone); made-up house state is unguarded (*The shed is closed tonight.*); once he ignored a gift on his card at the heart, and once (first pass) promised Truthcoin without the act.
4. The classic reflect estimate is 20478 of 20480 tokens: any persona or mind-file addition must trim or raise the orchestrator's `num_ctx`.
5. Guard 2's exact word pattern does not withhold a hyphenated or plural answer form in a reply (the memo scrub and ALREADY SAID match loosely).
6. A house line that uses a word the Kernel currently holds is dropped from `recent`: safe, but it can lose late-night context.
7. After a silent server restart the first live thought can carry a prefix reference the server never saw: the Mind page shows a dead reference until the next replay.
8. `traceAfter` is the `at` of the thought the server received last, not the largest: equivalent while one worker voices a character.
9. The server does not refuse a share outside the phase card's list; the worker is the only gate.
10. Classic was not re-run after the persona edits (the baseline; its prompt code is unchanged).
11. Post-ending lines keep the film (the ceremony's lines, the Recounts narration, the endings where he sings the line, The Heart's narration in the room): the user's call whether those go too.
12. Cosmetic: a `set drive` control's note reads *set drive: 61* without the drive's name.
13. Wave 11b's open issues 1 (the reflect budget: see 4), 3 and 5 stand; 2 (an answer in his voice prompt) and 4 (the *passing remark* bullet, 14c6c66) are closed.


### Wave 13 status (built 2026-10-20 on branches `oct31-w13/*`, merged into `oct31-w13/integration` at ccf4e45, not yet merged to `master` or shipped)

The user's asks, in their words (2026-10-20, from the day's UX mapping of the live show):
1. *"Rooms get noisy when mini-games are mixed with text."*
2. *"The typewriter takes too long entering a crowded room. Minimal for ordinary speech; exaggerated is great for effect text."*
3. *"The organisation of the app is bloated for guests and performers. The pass and the QR scanner must be easy to reach. Redo the top bar, especially on mobile."*
4. *"Pop-ups should be more annoying and frequent; announcements and beats get buried."*
5. Performers (the five Antivirus and Clippy): *"too many options per scan"*; clear pinned instructions, fewer options per interaction, and the story tracks the current act / beat / objectives / checklists.
6. Hosts (Adobe Acrobat, Recycling Bin, Task Manager): *"a super-charged but compact admin panel in the Play app; they shouldn't need to leave it all evening."*
7. (added) *"Better Codex + Quest Log UI: entries categorised, collapsed by default."*

**The user's decisions:** **D1** hosts get admin automatically by character (`CHARACTER … admin: true` on the three; whoever picks the part gets it; never Clippy; the passcode path stays). **D2** the interrupt dial is a Win95 ladder — balloon, sticky, dialog, blue screen — switchable live, with a glitch on every switch; minor moments one rung lower; the level lives at `Night.alerts` (header `alerts:`); `!!` takeovers stay above it. **D3** objectives are authored in the story (`OBJECTIVE`), drafted from the cast packs, for sign-off. **D4** the guest chrome is a bottom taskbar (⊞ Start, 🎟 Pass, 📷 Scan, the tray) and a NOW title bar on every screen. **D5** the host panel is a pull-up drawer with NEXT pinned (Cues · Needs · Judge · People · More); never restart, push draft, go live, end, the var table, log export or the Mind trace. **D6** it ships before Oct 31. **D7** the Codex and the Quest Log are categorised and collapsed. **D8** `admin: true` is the **host** capability, not full admin (the mod passcode in a booth still is). **D9** any host can fire NEXT; the server's 409 `moved` stops a double press and the drawer shows who pressed LAST. **D10** tap to arm, tap to fire (core's arm model). **D11** `CHANNEL system log` is deleted; the lobby is the one announcement home.

**What it is now.**
- **Guests** (Help → *Getting connected*, *Your pass*): the **NOW title bar** names the first live objective (tap → its card: brief, a checklist that ticks itself, up to three buttons), greys *(Paused)* while paused, red when captured, ochre when restricted. The **taskbar** holds ⊞ Start (the story's guest buttons first — 🚻 Bathroom Mode — then People, Help, picture, Keep my screen on, Get alerts, switching sides, Leave behind a confirm), **🎟 Pass** (full screen on white, the screen held awake) and **📷 Scan** (the camera with a typed-code box that also checks into a room by name), both one tap from every screen; the tray carries the story's guest countdown as a clock, the balance and **🔔 n** missed moments, with the live dot on its edge. Rooms keep a fixed order and fold empty ones under *Other rooms (n)*.
- **Rooms** (Help → *Messages & threads*, *Decisions*): a line crawls only if it arrives while its room is open (≤ 0.7 s a line, a 1.5 s burst budget, tap to finish); effect text (an effect manner, `crawl: fx`, alerts, takeovers) scrambles and flickers; answerable cards dock in a tray above the composer and settle into one line once answered; barks are a dim *Around the house* ticker; a run of `~` notices folds to one line; badges count only what is for you; a DM with a character that doesn't read replies has no composer. The crowded Desktop after three cues is readable 63 ms after opening (25–47 s before).
- **Moments** (Help → *Lore, people & private messages* → *Alerts and pop-ups*): one queue rendered on the story's rung, a glitch on every level change, dialogs and blue screens one at a time (*1 of 3*), *Take me there*, every missed moment replayed on wake (more than three as a list; an earlier phase's dropped), a booth never above sticky and a peek balloon over an open card.
- **Codex + Quest Log**: grouped by `category:` (every one of the show's 183 entries has one), one folded line each, a filter from four entries, NEW until opened; quests In progress first with the latest step, Done / Failed folded.
- **Booths** (Help → *Performer station*): the **NOW card** pinned on top (title on two lines, three to-dos in view, story ticks greyed, a hand tick saved per phone), **📷 Scan** the biggest button; a scan opens the card at once — facts by severity, **at most three face buttons** from the live objectives (the first framed as the default), the story's reply / what the character said / the `# hosts` refusal on the card, *All buttons… (n)* — and 📷 Next guest keeps the camera; staff rooms pinned; **Readouts** persisted (core moves a booth readout to the character's `dm:` Inbox); a guest's typed DM surfaces under **Messages**; a double-tapped scan is one scan.
- **Hosts** (Help → *The host console*): the **NEXT bar** above the taskbar (ARM → FIRE, PICK for a subject, the strip: act · phase · ⏸/🚪 · countdown · ⚠ needs; a refusal's reason; *Last update n s ago* when stale) and the console — **Cues** (LAST and who pressed it, by name), **Needs** (`hostNeeds`, shared with the editor), **Judge**, **People** (open card, 🚻 end Bathroom Mode, ⛔, 👋, 🔁 re-pair, 📡 scan as), **More** (🚪 Open the doors / ⏸ Pause / ▶ Resume, Announce, the alert dial, wallet holds, the King, terminals, codes + the join and invitation QRs + 🔓, parts, answering as Trabolta). Server side: the host capability on the session (`/api/prime/login` of an `admin: true` part; re-derived on every story load), the host allowlist (`core/server/routes/host.ts`; everything else 403 *not from a booth*), `GET /api/state?role=mod&host=1` (`hostView`, coalesced to one build per 2 s), `POST /api/prime/card`, `card` / `replies` / `staff` / `said` on `/api/scan` and `/api/prime/act`, journaled `Booth · <Character>`.
- **The story** (`objectives.loom`, 69 objectives; `SIGNOFF-w13.md`): `admin: true` on the three hosts; `alerts: Night.alerts` (sticky at Power On, dialog at ⏳ and 🕚, blue screen at 🌑, sticky at 🏓 and 🏆); effect manners on the Truther Download and ten lines; 23+ `~` notices with their personal replies; `moment: major` on the bottle's card and the pickup line; new `!` alerts for the beats that got buried; the ROLE watch line for the booth card's facts; codex categories; tighter `while:` on the always-on buttons; 🛡 ✅ 👁 `by: Antivirus`; 🩹 only on a marked program's card; every to-do ticks; `# system-log` deleted.
- **The editor**: the performer lens is the booth's own `PrimeView` (no admin cues), readouts off the Inbox, the Inspector's *Their view* / *Their booth* list objectives, a host part's lens and Players pane are a host's, and `@loom/core/host-board` (the judging, wallet, parts, unseen-guest and award helpers, `hostNeeds`) is shared with the play app.
- **The language** (Help → *Objectives*, *Live shows*, *Broadcasts*, *Cards & widgets*, *The Codex*; the writer's guide §11.13b–§11.14): `OBJECTIVE`, `admin:`, `crawl: fx`, effect manners, header `alerts:`, the `~` notice, CODEX `category:`, `show … with moment:`; lints L1205 (a ROLE `secret:` slot on a watch item), L1207–L1212.

**Built:** Stage A `core` (b2305e1) and `plumbing` (0f10776, play split into lane modules) on `master`, then on `oct31-w13/integration`: Stage B **story** (facaac7), **stream** (596a6ec), **chrome** (8cb5704), **moments** (55b8c0a), **booth** (e6e175e), **host** (a21f18d), **editor** (1c20a44), each built, reviewed and fixed; integration **icore** (a4460fa: `PrimeView.paused` / `host`, the phase stamp on moments, who pressed LAST), **istory** (e0017e4: ⊞ Start wording, the tour's copy into the sign-off sheet, one post per broadcast), **iwire** (95ec44f: moments mounted for guests and booths), **ifloor** (3408c23: the 44 px floor, the browser floor); a live round (guest, booths, hosts, adversary, load, plain + embed) and a finish review; the fix round **fperf** (016d341), **fapi** (6ee71f0), **fstory** (74f5003), **fbooth** (4310f4b), **fhost** (c0d0490), **fhostby** (7162b71) and **fguest** (ccf4e45); this docs branch.

**Numbers** (no-DB scratch servers, the show, seeded K-32 mixes; `w12-1019` = the baseline):

|---|---|---|---|

| server CPU mean, late night | 25 % | 28 % | **15 %** |
| booth / host snapshot, late night, under load | — | 49.0 / 45.3 KB | **31.8 / 28.1 KB** |

**Suites on `oct31-w13/integration` after fix round C:** core 2118 (123 files, incl. the Postgres-gated suites) + `tsc` clean, play 888 (97 files) + typecheck + build, editor 456 (56 files) + `tsc -b`, terminal 245; loomcheck `--smoke` 0 / 0; `titi:facts --check` up to date. Not re-run since master (untouched): house, reinstall/web, chess, bank, stagehand, VR.

**Decisions taken for the user in the fix rounds (veto any):**
1. **Hosts may open the doors, pause and resume from Play** (More → ⏯ The show, armed then fired): `/api/mod/start` and `/api/mod/stop` joined the host allowlist. Restart, reload, push draft, go live and end stay off it.
2. **The 🗂 Guests badge counts guests waiting on an unread message**, not the guest count (CONTRACTS §9.7 said the count; it read as nine unread alerts). The total is the button's tooltip; a guest's typed DM to the booth shows under **Messages** (also a departure from §9.7's "no per-guest rows").
3. **🛡 Send to the Internet arms then fires on every Antivirus booth and is off Windows Defender's face** (fix round C, Q26: a one-tap capture of a just-cleared late arrival was too easy); Windows Defender's scan of a marked program sends them to a room Antivirus (`holds_the_door`).
4. **A ribbon after Bingo is announced as *from the hosts*** (Q28): 🎀 is every host's, and the story can't tell who pressed it.
5. **`loud:` only on Show Your Pass** (Q14): every other loud candidate already raises an `!` alert from the same cue, which at `dialog` would be two modals for one beat.
6. **A host is offered another character's `by:` button only when its press is heard** (a story rule or a ROLE hook; fhostby): the server room's 🔌 💥 ❌ 🚪 🔧 stay on a host's card, 🛡 ✅ 👁 and Acrobat's 🫘 leave it, and the server refuses them (Q2, Q22). A headless moderator's press is never refused as unheard.
7. **`LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS` is recommended for show night**, pending the recheck's measurement of a late-night guest snapshot (54 KB, unchanged by fperf) over uncompressed SSE.
8. Also taken in the lanes: with no live objective the NOW bar names the open room, else the story's title (D4); a booth has no separate title bar (the NOW card is it); the lobby is exempt from the moments' same-room staleness rule (every `!` lands there); a duplicate of the same moment within 2 story-seconds is one; a readout about the guest whose card is open shows on the card, not as a moment; `GuestView.night.act` is null (only booths read it); a lookalike of a taken guest name is refused (NFKC + invisible characters); a code over 64 characters is refused before the journal; a scan repeated within 1.5 s answers 429 `again` and journals nothing.

**Deploy order** (ships on its own on top of `w12-1019`, the rollback):
1. Merge `oct31-w13/integration` + `oct31-w13/docs` to `master` (`--no-ff`), then on the Mac `scripts/ops/predeploy.sh --vr` → `scripts/ops/ship.sh w13-10xx`. **The server first.** The proxy image carries `deploy/Caddyfile`'s `lb_try_duration 60s` (a full-journal restart takes ≈ 32 s). Hashed `/assets/*` are now immutable for a year and HTML revalidates. No migration, no map change, no new required `.env` key; set `LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS` if the recheck says so.
2. **`sync-show` WITH its reload, before doors** (never `--no-reload`; no `--replace-maps`): the composition changed — no `# system-log`, `~` notices, booth readouts in each character's Readouts, one post per broadcast — so the chat seqs move, and a wave 12 journal must never play the wave 13 text. Restart any other non-ended event.
3. Reload the terminals, the TV and the tent tablet once the server is back; every phone and booth reloads itself on its next open.
4. After the sign-off, reprint the cast packs (`cast-packs/`: every performer pack, `all-crew.md`, the director card, `patch-bay-watch.md`, the README's notes) and the director's card. Nothing changes on the house Mac (stagehand untouched) or the headset.

**Open issues after wave 13:**
1. **The show text awaits the user's sign-off:** `SIGNOFF-w13.md` Q1–Q28 and §2 (the 69 objectives), §3 (every other line), §6 (delivery notes now shown), §8 (the tour's and the app's own words). Q1 — who calls the cues: the second director at Run, or a host from Play — decides the hosts' packs.
2. The bank, the Rust engine and the TextMate grammar don't know `OBJECTIVE`, `admin:`, `crawl:`, `alerts:`, `~`, `category:` or `with moment:` (nothing on the night runs through them).
3. Not device-checked: the iOS keyboard with the folded tray and the stepping-aside taskbar; a camera kept running hidden under a card on iOS Safari (and `play()` waking it on Next guest); the old Android tablets on the `:has()` / `color-mix()` fallbacks; a real camera and the held wake lock under the full-screen pass.
4. A late-night guest snapshot is still ≈ 54 KB over uncompressed SSE (1.5 Mbit/s a phone at 45 guests): `LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS` (decision 7) or compressing the guest stream.
5. The ~31 s late-night replay: under Caddy's new 60 s hold, but a restart mid-show is still half a minute of *reconnecting…*.
6. *"Who pressed LAST"* names a host or an author; a headless mod-token press reads *Director*, and the editor's NOW card doesn't read the name yet.
7. A to-do with no `done when` on a guest's objective stays open (none left in the show); a hand tick in a booth lives only on that phone.
8. `fire open the doors` in a beat body reads as prose (an event name that starts with a built-in verb word, the known rule); L1212 catches only the two-word trigger case.

## 9. The workaround runbook

Every 🩹 item in the checklist points at one row here. **Who** is the role on the night (§10). The full build is in SCHEDULE.md for after the party.

| W | Replaces (WP · checklist ids) | Procedure on the night | Who | Cue / when |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W-01** | Identities survive a restart; reload-keep-run (WP-02 part, WP-65 · SW-QR-17, SW-ADM-14) | **Morning of 10-31, before anyone registers: sync, Push draft, check, then Go live in place** (Go live restarts from the running snapshot, so the Push draft is what puts the frozen story on stage; §3). After doors: never Restart, Push draft, Go live or End (each wipes guests). A server *process* restart is safe (journals replay, tokens kept; since wave 8 a director's broadcasts, Trabolta's power counts, his unanswered DMs and the TV mute come back too; since wave 9 Caddy holds requests through it and every phone, booth, Run console and tablet reconnects by itself, and `sync-show` refuses to reload a live event with real guests, exit 3). It takes ≈ 1 s per 1,500 journal lines (a full night ≈ 10 s), and **anyone mid-reinstallation is scanned in again** (the walk's session ends). Fix a story bug live with director fires + Run → World edits | Director | Morning reset → wrap |
| **W-02** | The map belongs to the project (WP-03 · SW-MAP-01, SW-MAP-06) | **Retired by Oct 31 wave 2 (SW-MAP-01 built):** the surveyed map is the project's `house.json`, pushed by `sync-show`; a map-page edit is kept with **Save to project** (the sync keeps the project's copy); *Download JSON* → commit → `sync-show --replace-maps` makes the repo copy the show's. A relaunch never carries a director's save, and a variant switch or Save to project retires one — but **Push draft and Go live in place keep one** (same event, same state dir), so a rehearsal's save still outranks the project's map on the night. Still: never save in `/reinstall/map` on show day without Save to project, and at the morning reset check the map page's status line reads "the project's map" (`source: project`). If it reads "this event's map": **Save to project** to keep the edit, or switch to the other variant and back to drop it | Tech op | Morning reset |

## 10. Night-of crew + cheat-sheets

**Minimum crew:** 1 director at Run (the **second director**, since John plays Task Manager) + 1 **tech operator** (not in the story, stays at the TV computer) + 1 **house manager** (not performing) + Clippy + 3–4 Antivirus (one **stationed in the server room from Y2K**) + 3 hosts + Julia at the tent (the Installation Wizard's voice, no booth) + a **tent assistant / VR spotter** if the Quest is a go + a door/porch person until ~20:30 (Recycling Bin). Names for the three new crew roles by **Tue 10-20**.

The **house manager** is the only free pair of hands: door host from 20:30 and the late arrivals (OP-18), the loaner phones and re-pair runner, **the safety lead during the blackout** and any `ALL STOP` (OP-20), the 911 caller and the neighbour liaison.

Every role gets **one laminated card** (D-01 drafts, PAPER prints in batch 2). What's on each:

| Card | Holder | On the card |
|---|---|---|
| **Director** | Second director (John covers off-stage moments) | Screen: Run → Director (cue sheet), Run → Mind in a 2nd tab (its Judging card: `auto` / `review`, overrides), the K-29 card, `# hosts` for 📝 filings. **Cue order:** C1 → V2 → V3 → Clippy 2/X (radio "INTRO" first) → V4/V5 → (auto C2) → V6 → ⏱ → 📋 (×3) → 🍾 (×5 per question) → 📺 → 📓 → (auto 📥 ⏳ 🚨) → 🏓 → 🧮 → 📊 → 🏆 → 🫘 → 🗜 → 🤖 → (radio **"IN"** from the patch-bay watch: inside, door shut) → 🕚 → (auto majority / N-06) → door/patch (or Tier 0) → 🏍 or 🪦 (or auto 🌑) → 🍎 → 📄 → endings → (✍ if the award lines are ours) → ceremony. **Set stage** clicks (W-21). **The server judges filings** (wave 4); override from the Judging card (since wave 9 every judged filing has ✅ / 😤 / 🤷 there, in both modes); a host's 📝 judges a program's latest filing only. **NEXT is empty while a timer owns the night** (hunt 1, hunt 2's hour + the summons, the Y2K hour) and a cue pressed a step early is refused in `# hosts` (wave 9). **Never** Restart / Push draft / Go live / End after doors, no personas. Keep the Run tab in the foreground (a background tab throttles the K-29 chime). Fixes: stuck codex → printed list · stuck quest → fire the event · stale `afk` → it times out at 15 min (World if needed) · ghost majority → N-06 · dead Server Door terminal → ♟ `chess won` naming the winner (the door code) · King's bar wrong → 👑 or Set… on Run → House map (OP-24) · crew through the server-room door → 🔧 first (OP-23) · ⚠ near miss in `# hosts` → judge it on the Judging card before 📋 · a guest who left → 👋 Went home · the door opened and nothing happened → 🚪 (The Heart) · `# hosts` "🕊 Trabolta offers to stand down" → your call: 🕊 · 🔄 reset after a forgiven wrong patch (then 💡 if the house is still red) · dead bay → OP-13 · lost guest → **N-02 re-pair QR** · **late arrival → Inspector → scan as Norton (OP-18)** · **performer lost → Release (N-10, OP-19)** · mute the TV voice → K-29 card · `ALL STOP` → 💡 Restore the house lights (OP-20) |

Watch: Z2M frontend, stagehand log, K-29, QLab. Owns: the blackout safety check (with the house manager), the ad-hijack video, the show-night backup check (OP-21), **the keypad lock test at 15:00** (outside with the code, inside with the handle and no code; OP-23), the server-room terminal (asleep until The Heart) and the Server Door terminal (awake all night on THE KING). **Never `pkill -f "stagehand run"`** |
| **Clippy** | Brooke | Buttons by phase: 🎱 + 🎀 + 🎯 named on the card (V3) · ✅/😤 Q1–3 for spoken answers + 🤷 (V6; filings are judged by the server, the 📝 buttons override) · 🗣 / 🙅 / 🎩 at the bottle (🍾, five picks a question; 🙅 and 🎩 mark) · ✅/😤 Q1–2 (final reveal) · 🍎 · 💘. Her booth has a Quest Log (*Run The Bingo*, *Host The Hunt*, *Keep Everyone Calm*) and a Codex. **Q numbers are within the round** (H1–H3 · H4–H6 · F1–F2). Accurate and confident-lie look identical in public. Judge by scanning the answerer's pass. Cues to perform: C1 welcome, Bingo rules, Corrupted Code, the Trabolta intro, **panic at the ad (CI 4)**, beans = malware, final reveal, Purgatory (CI 9), host the ceremony after `destroyed`. Printed CI 1–9 cards |

Buttons: 🛡 Send to the Internet (any time) · 🩹 Unmark · ✅ Mark as reinstalled · 📍 Checked in · 🪙 Pay/Charge (McAfee/CRA) · ♟ only after **seeing** the mate. **Marked programs (wave 4):** a `!` chime + a 🚨 escort pin in `# quarantine` names who to walk to the Internet and why; 🛡 (or 🩹) clears it. **CRA:** the Wallet room in the booth — every third transfer waits for **Approve**, any transfer can be **Denied** within 60 s. Quest Log + Codex in every Antivirus booth. Norton: the selected Antivirus at V3 (interrupt the centre square). Password Manager: logs faction joins at Y2K. Sweeps: C1, C2, ⏳, the late list, Trabolta's sweep requests, 🚨 the server-room alarm (wave 7: only the second wrong patch-bay combination; whoever is at the cables goes; the door never alarms and anyone with the code belongs inside), 🗜. **Blackout: guide people to the living room; the stairs stay lit** |

All hosts: 📝 accurate / confident lie / uncertain override the server's verdict. **Adobe Acrobat: pong judge** (🏓 + ✅/😤/🤷; **press 🏓 Made the cup for every sink** — since wave 5 a missed press costs that program its round-2 filings), then by the printer (🫘 on her own booth, else radio "BEANS"), holds the Bag of Truth + a dry truth.txt, 🍎 with Clippy. Recycling Bin: door/porch until C1 (featured-name watch, OP-01/02), Disk Cleanup attendant. All: 💘 in Grok Prom, 🎀 ribbons |

There is no chess on the tablet: the board is the Server Door terminal by the real door. A marked arrival is captured when their run begins (`reinstallation begun`), so a missed 🛡 is fine. ## 11. Show day: call times + pre-show checklist

**Sat Oct 31.** Sunset ≈ 18:10 ADT, typically 4–11 °C, wind and rain common. Trick-or-treating ≈ 18:00–20:00. **DST ends 02:00 Nov 1**: the story uses elapsed time and doesn't care; the backup cron runs in UTC; tell the crew the wall clock jumps back, and **plan rides home** (transit is thin after 01:00).

| Time | Who | What |
|---|---|---|
| 10:00 | Director + tech op | **Tent/shed confirmation** from the forecast (**gusts > 30–40 km/h** or steady rain → shed, W-22). **Morning reset**: sync, **Push draft**, check the door QR on cellular, then **Go live in place** (W-01), before anyone registers |
| 12:00 | Set crew | Final set dressing, tent pitched or shed set, glow tape checked |
| 15:00 | Tech op | Boot everything (tech card); systems check below |
| 17:00 | **Performer call** + house manager | Costumes; phones charged; booths signed in **after go-live** with the prime code + cast picker; push subscribed (K-31a, iPhones via K-31b); a test `!` alert to staff; walk the stations |
| 18:00 | Door host | Porch set (OP-01); Optical Drive dim; pre-show checklist signed off |
| 19:00 | Everyone | Food + 15-min briefing: the blackout rule, call words incl. **ALL STOP** and **ARRIVAL**, the emergency card, "never restart". Tech op starts the 10-min backup job (OP-21) |
| 19:45 | Everyone | Places. Lights to screensaver |
| **20:00** | | **Doors** (rolling arrivals; featured guests first wave) |
| **≤ 21:00** | Director | **C1** (OP-17) |
| ≈ 01:45 | | Ending + ceremony done |

**Pre-show checklist** (the director ticks it, K-08 prints it):

1. Deploy is the pinned `loom-server:show-1031` / `loom-proxy:show-1031`; the previous image is still on the VPS; **the warm standby exists (stopped)**; the 10-min off-box backup job is armed for 19:00 (OP-21); uptime alerts on `https://app.mapsandducks.com/api/health` (alert on anything but 200) reach the tech op + second director, and the sheet printed with `scripts/ops/checklist.sh --probe | lpr` heads with a ✔ health line (`{"ok":true,"controlPlane":true,"events":N}`; a ⚠ line is fixed before doors).
2. **Sync + Push draft, then Go live in place, done before any registration**; codes are the printed ones (scan the door QR from a phone on cellular).
3. The map page's *Map:* bar shows **Tent** or **Shed** (the one chosen at 10:00) as `● live`, and its status line reads "the project's map" (`GET /api/spatial/house` says `source: project`). If it reads "this event's map", a rehearsal's director save is still in force (Push draft and Go live in place keep it): press **Save to project** to keep that edit, or switch to the other variant and back to drop it.
4. Trabolta: worker connected (Run → Mind status, a *mind · story* chip: `mind_mode: new`), **mind Reset**, `ollama ps` shows `gpt-oss:20b` alone, `lookup: false` (on classic only: thinking off, **mind paused** unless both models fit, OP-06); the laptop on its charger with `caffeinate`; he's hidden and asleep.
5. Terminals: every tablet green on K-29, on `view=auto` showing attract (the Server Door terminal on `view=chess` showing THE KING); the TV on `view=tv` showing the screensaver, **clicked once after its last boot**.
6. Lights: Z2M + WLED up; `lights_screensaver` look on; one `lights_blackout` test: the stairs, bathroom, tent, server room and the path to the living room stay lit, the tube strip dim; battery path lights armed; the spare dongle + Z2M backup on the shelf.
7. Door + bay: the door contact reads open/closed in the stagehand log; the keypad opens from outside with the story's code (`facts.json` door.code) and from inside with the handle and no code; the ring shows locked; the patch bay is all unplugged (reset).
8. Audio: QLab show file open, one test cue per zone at show level; the house-announcement player (the stagehand `alexa` module) running.
9. Push: the staff test alert arrived on every performer phone.
10. Tent: tablet charged and on `/reinstall/` (the King visible at the server-room door, his bar 15 / 15), the Installation Wizard video loaded on the 2nd device, Quests charged (if go), heater on its GFCI leg, EXIT sign on.
11. The Server Door terminal by the keypad on `view=chess`, charging, green on K-29, THE KING at 15 / 15 (wave 11: Run → House map's King row; 👑 if a rehearsal left him lower). Bean printer button paired.
12. Print: codex posters up + clue props hidden (photos match), the four silver digit notes hidden in their rooms (wave 11), the invitations sent with their claim QRs, check-in signs up, Bingo cards + pens, the fact stacks hidden in the Desktop's print media (photos match `generated/print-stacks.md`), trophies, cheat-sheets.
13. Safety: first aid + extinguisher placed, egress clear, glow tape, GFCIs tested (A-18), no strobe > 3 Hz, the server door opens from inside with the handle and no code, batteries in or out (nothing in the YAML drives a lock), the patch-bay gear taped off, the emergency card posted at the door.
14. Loaner phones (2–3) charged; the guest charging station live; power banks charged for every performer; radios charged with earpieces.
15. The backup Mac is on, with the QLab file + media, next to the TV computer with the HDMI adapter; the ISP router is boxed nearby as the cold spare.
16. Facts + wallet (wave 4): `titi:facts --check` passed at predeploy and the synced project carries the team's `facts.json`; Run → Mind's Judging card reads `auto`; a test phone shows the Wallet room and a 🪙 balance; `# quarantine` is empty.
17. Knobs (waves 5 + 9): on the VPS, `cd ~/loom && deploy/release.sh --check-env` ends `env: the night's knobs are set ✔` (LOOM_RL_RESOLVE=600, LOOM_RL_REGISTER=400, LOOM_RL_LOGIN_FAILURES=100/300, both VAPID keys set, LOOM_BASE_URL=https://app.mapsandducks.com); `grep -E '^LOOM_(SNAPSHOT_MS|HOMESCREEN)=' ~/loom/.env` shows both blank, unless the K-32 run failed (then `LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS=500`) or the K-31b call was yes (then `LOOM_HOMESCREEN=on`); the print sheet's door sign matches the Home Screen switch; Run → Wallet names CRA Agent Software as the approver and Runescape as the minter.
18. Wave 6: on the house Mac, `uv run stagehand check --config laptop.yaml --only agents` shows `ctx 20480` on Trabolta's mind line and no warning; every wall terminal's ⚙ names `Trabolta` and none sits on "The story has no character called …"; on a Shed night the SITE-SCHEDULE clipboard and the BUGDOM-VR sketch hang in The Optical Drive (the mud room).
19. Wave 7: the keypad lock was set by hand to facts.json door.code and tested at 15:00 (OP-23), and it opens from inside with no code; the keypad sign hangs beside it; the server-room terminal is green on the Terminals card and asleep on its screensaver; Run → Director shows 🔄 reset and 🕊 talked down; the reprinted M22 card reads CHECKMATE-CODE. Wave 11: the keypad sign reads FOUR DIGITS. FOUR NOTES ARE HIDDEN IN THE HOUSE (no riddle).

**Safety one-pager** (walked Fri 10-30, re-checked at 18:00):

- **Light.** Neither the blackout nor Trabolta's `cut_the_lights` darkens the stairs, bathroom, tent, server room or the server-room → living-room path. Trabolta can't cut The Cloud (backyard); the kitchen only dims to ≈ 20%. The tent-path flood is on a plug in no group. The safety bulbs are `power_on_behavior: on`, so a wall switch always brings them back. Battery motion lights on every stair and path. Glow tape on every step. Staff carry headlamps.
- **Flashing.** Every flashing preset is ≤ 3 Hz (stopwatch-timed 10-22). No full-field saturated red. The finished media passed **PEAT**. A warning is on the invitation and the door.
- **Power.** Everything outdoors or near water is on a GFCI (tested), with **12 AWG** outdoor cords fully uncoiled, drip loops, and connections in a dry box off the ground. The radiator runs on low, ≥ 1 m from the fabric, on its own circuit (the breaker map is taped by the panel). LED PSUs are closed and fused per branch, off carpet. **No combustion heaters in or near the tent.**
- **Weather and tent.** Move to the shed on gusts > 30–40 km/h. VR is played seated with a spotter.
- **Doors and gear.** The server-room door has a keypad lock on the outside only: from inside the handle always opens with no code, batteries or not, and nothing in the house can lock anyone in (no lock rules exist in the YAML; its test forbids them). The override key is with the house manager. The real gear is out of guests' reach.
- **Fire.** First aid + ABC extinguisher near the PSUs and the TV computer. LED candles only. The Tube fabric is flame-retardant. A towel + mat under the bobbing tub, away from the stairs.
- **Outside the house.** Trick-or-treaters never wander into a dark Optical Drive (the mud room). The backyard (Z3) goes quiet late (Truth Pong ≈ 23:30), and the neighbours have been told. Rides home are planned; the clock falls back at 02:00.

## 12. Risk register

| Risk | Early sign | Fallback on the night | Rehearsed |
|---|---|---|---|
| **House uplink (WAN) lost** | K-29 cards red; stagehand log disconnects | LTE failover on the GL-MT3000 (tethered phone, a plan that allows tethering, L-18). Guests are on cellular and keep playing. QLab is local, so audio + lights hold their last look. **But no Act III automation fires while the WAN is down**: door → The Heart (wave 7; was door → alarm), patch combos → alarm / blackout and the house announcements all go ESP32 → stagehand → VPS → story → back. Stagehand + the worker reconnect (K-05); re-fire the current lights cue (OP-16). Any outage that lasts past the failover → Tier 0 (W-31/W-32), QLab by hand, Z2M frontend scenes by hand | WAN pull 10-28 |
| **VPS down** | The uptime check pages **the tech op and the second director** (John is on stage) | `docker compose restart` (journals replay; tokens kept; ≈ 10 s late in the night; rescan anyone mid-reinstallation). Bad deploy → the one-command rollback (K-08). Host dead → start the **warm standby** (built 10-30, stopped), restore the **newest 10-min copy** from the house laptop (OP-21; last night's backup predates the show), repoint DNS (TTL 300 s) | Restore drill 10-21 |
| **Trabolta's model / worker down** | Run → Mind shows the worker offline | N-11 (wave 1): with no worker online the hub posts Trabolta's authored `hold:` line ("Jean Trabolta is not responding…") after 20 s, and queues the request for up to 10 min; a director's reply as Trabolta from Run closes the queued request. Since wave 11 a worker that stays connected but stops answering is **stalled** after 60 s: one ⏳ line in `# hosts`, the hold line once per waiting thread, no new dispatch until it answers or reconnects. The tech op restarts `--only agents`; the laptop never sleeps (`caffeinate`, charger, `OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE=-1`). No hosted fallback (decision #35 = no). Scripted story lines still play; the director answers key DMs as Trabolta from Run; Clippy's "high volume" line; the TV plays the pre-rendered speeches (P-3) if they were made | 10-25 |
| **Agent overload** (30 guests at once) | Voice queue > 1 min | Wave 12's story mind: one call per line, no orchestrator to swap in (K-04: 30 DMs in 83–87 s); the switch ladder if it misbehaves. On classic, OP-06 (mandatory): the mind paused by default, thinking off; one Ollama = 60–180 s for 30 DMs, and each voice ⇄ orchestrator swap reloads for ≈ 25 s unless both models fit (I-8); Clippy's "high volume" line | K-04 burst test |
| **Kokoro slow or down** *(moot since wave 2: the devices speak with their own voices and Kokoro is opt-in; the row applies only if you opt in)* | TV lags > 3 s | The TV falls back to Chrome's voice (its Chrome runs with `--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required`; after any reboot someone clicks once into the page, or it stays silent). N-12 (Kokoro on the TV Mac) if built. **The tablets may have no browser voice at all** (Fully Kiosk's WebView, K-02): for them the pre-rendered P-3 files are the real fallback. Kokoro has a `cpus:` limit so it can't starve the event server | 10-25 |
| **TV computer dies** (one box runs the TV, QLab, Mosquitto, Z2M, stagehand) | Screen black, lights frozen | UPS + never-sleep prevent most of it; drinks kept away. Reboot (launchd brings everything back; one click into the TV page). If dead: **the named backup Mac**, pre-loaded with the QLab workspace, media and pre-rendered files, goes onto the TV's HDMI via the USB-C adapter and opens Chrome `view=tv`; lights hold their last look and the blackout is skipped (the director narrates); door/bay Tier 0 | 10-28 (reboot test + swap) |
| **Quest fails** | 10-22 call, or a headset dies | W-33: web game on 2 tablets in the tent | 10-25 / 10-29 |
| **Patch bay not finished or flaky** | 10-22 bench fails; double fires | W-31: crew watches, director fires `patch_combo_a` / `patch_combo_b` / `patch_rejected` from Run | 10-28 |
| **Door sensor flaky** | ~~False alarms on reconnect~~ wave 7: The Heart misfires, or never fires (a broken closes-on-open loop is silent) | `edge: true` + ignore-retained + `debounce_s: 20` (K-05); the story's 120 s quiet window after each opening. Else W-32: the director fires 🚪 when the crew see the door open | 10-22, 10-28 |
| **The keypad lock fails** (wave 7: a dead battery, a jam, a code that doesn't match `facts.json`) | The 15:00 test (OP-23); a guest with the code who can't get in | W-35: the override key, prop the door, 🚪. Egress never depends on it (the inside handle) | 10-28 |
| **An exit re-triggers the cutscene** (wave 7: the contact is direction-blind) | The Heart / *Back again* on someone stepping out | The story stays quiet for 120 s after the last opening; *Back To The Heart* is one short line; 🔄 reset re-arms The Heart only on purpose | 10-28 |
| **Push not working** | 10-21 Android check or the 10-24 call fails | `!` alerts in the app (K-06) + the house announcements (K-20) + screen overlays (K-17) + Antivirus patrols + Clippy's "keep Trapped open, screen on" + N-09 (terminals speak) if built | 10-25 phones in pockets |
| **Old tablets fail** (no WebGL2, crash, sleep) | K-02 tags; K-29 red | Face view (K-14); Fully Kiosk restart on crash; the spare tablet; heartbeat alerts the director | 10-28 |
| **iPhone Antivirus can't scan** | N-01 not verified | Android phones at the door (OP-04); type the id | 10-25 |
| **Guest loses their session** | A guest at the director asking | N-02 re-pair QR in < 30 s; a loaner phone | 10-25 |
| **Ghost guests block the majority** | Y2K head count < `count(Persons)` | Wave 11: 👋 Went home on Run (the majority counts only present programs; personas never count); then N-06 `force_majority_stay` / `force_majority_leave` (OP-11) | 10-29 |
| **Restart / End pressed by mistake** | Everyone's phone shows the join screen | Prevention: only the two director consoles are signed in (the director's laptop + the second director's own account as a backup console); the cheat-sheet says never. If it happens: everyone rescans the door QR and re-picks their program; the director replays the current phase cue and restores key vars in Run → World. Expensive, survivable | Briefing |
| **Hunt answers leak through Trabolta** | A DM where he states an H-answer | `lookup: false` + K-23 + the secrecy test; the director mutes or pauses the mind | K-23 test |
| **Weather** | 10-29 forecast | Shed (W-22), decided 10-29, confirmed 10:00 | 10-29 drill |
| **Trick-or-treaters in the dark Optical Drive (mud room)** | 18:00–20:00 | OP-01 porch | 10-30 |
| **Staffing gap** (John double-cast) | No names by 10-20 | Second director + tech op (OP-15); else cut John's Task Manager lines to a host | 10-25 |
| **Wrong URL / code on the prints** | K-07 | Codes pinned (K-10); the test sheet on cellular before batch 1; never press End | 10-21 |
| **Blackout unsafe** (stairs dark, strobe) | 10-22 rehearsal | Today's YAML darkens the stairs: `lights_blackout` hits `house` + `wled/all`, and the `tube` group is the hall + stair landing. K-05 takes the safety rooms out of `house` and turns WLED off per device; `power_on_behavior: on`; battery path lights; presets rebuilt ≤ 3 Hz; `ALL STOP` (OP-20); the house manager leads | 10-22, 10-28 |
| **Video on the TV doesn't layer** (QLab over Chrome) | 10-25 | N-04a (TV plays the MP4); else the PNG still + the jingle | 10-25 |
| **Featured name taken by a stranger** | Door host sees it | Wave 11: reserved, so only the claim QR or a director's 2-minute 🔓 takes it (W-03); else OP-02 | — |
| **Software slips past 10-27** | Any lane behind at 10-23 | The cut order (§13) | — |
| **The show runs a stale story** | `stale: true` on the event; a fixed bug reappears | Evening sync + Push draft 10-16 → 10-30; morning sync + Push draft + Go live on 10-31 (§3, W-01) | 10-30 evening, 10-31 morning |
| **The server can't keep up** (≈ 62 clients; a full snapshot per client per step) | K-32 p95 > 1 s, CPU > 70% | Coalesce guest + booth snapshots to ≤ 2/s (10-24 buffer); K-22 for house clients; 8 vCPU VPS | K-32 10-23 |
| **Door rush locks out house Wi-Fi** (one shared IP) | 429s on registration at 20:00 | K-11's env-raised limits; guests on cellular | 10-25 |
| **Merge conflicts across lanes** (`event-runtime.ts`, Run/Inspector) | A lane's rebase fails in the evening | Routes in `core/server/routes/<id>.ts`; merge order B → A → C → D; rebase before hand-in (§3) | Daily |
| **Router dies** | Every house device drops at once | The ISP router as a cold spare with the **same SSID/PSK**; the TV computer's IP is set on the machine and hard-coded in the ESP32 firmware (no DHCP dependence); the ISP modem/ONT on the UPS | 10-28 |
| **Z2M coordinator dies** | Z2M frontend shows the bridge offline | Swap in the spare dongle (same model) + restore the `data/` backup (tested 10-23). If the restore fails, re-pairing takes ≈ 1 h: wall switches for the night (the safety bulbs come back ON) | 10-23 restore |
| **Director laptop dies** | Run gone | The second director's own signed-in laptop (a project member since 10-20); the printed cue order | 10-25 |
| **A performer loses their phone** | Booth dead; "Clippy can't sign in" (409) | N-10 release + re-sign on a loaner in < 60 s (OP-19); power banks at the charging station | 10-25 |
| **Late arrivals stuck in the Optical Drive** | Roster: *uploaded, not cleared* after C1 | OP-18: Inspector → scan as Norton; "ring and wait" sign | 10-29 |
| **Tent ↔ shed switch does nothing** | The duck stays in the tent map | The variant switch on `/reinstall/map` or `POST /api/mod/spatial/variant` (W-22, wave 2); check `source` in `GET /api/spatial/house`; last resort: paste the shed JSON into the JSON tab and save | 10-29 drill |
| **Nobody finds the code** (wave 7; was "chess disarm unwinnable") | Nobody beats the King, nobody holds all four notes, no majority | Wave 11: the four digit notes, scannable all night (the keypad sign and *The Cold Door* point at them, and notes can be shared); the King at the Server Door terminal all night, weaker with every hit in the games (0 = pawns only; the director can lower his bar with Set…); the majority's Whole Truth; else a host who saw a mate presses ♟, or the director fires ♟ naming a winner | 10-25 |
| **The King's bar falls too early** (wave 11: one bar for the night) | Run → House map's King row low before the Y2K hour | That is the design (an easy game late); if it is too easy too soon, 👑 restore or Set… a value (OP-24); a headset player behind his door keeps it open | 10-25 |
| **Physical injury** (stairs, tent, kitchen, VR) | — | The safety one-pager (§11); the house manager is the safety lead; the emergency card (911, QEII Halifax Infirmary); first aid kit | 10-28 `ALL STOP` drill |

**Decision #35, answered 2026-10-15: no.** (N-11 built in wave 1; the directors answer DMs from Run.) The question was: **may guest DMs to Trabolta go to a hosted model as a fallback?** If yes, `agents.fallback.yaml` points the same persona at a hosted OpenAI-compatible endpoint that any laptop can run, as zero-code insurance if the Ollama laptop dies. It does send guests' DM text to a third party. **Default if unanswered: no.** Build N-11 instead (the hold line from the hub), and the director answers key DMs.

## 13. Cut order if we slip

Cut from the top. Each cut is **revert or switch off**, never half-ship. Decide at the end of each day from 10-23 on; anything not verified by **10-27 18:00** is cut automatically.

| # | Cut | Saves | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Optional items still unbuilt (N-12, S3) | 0.25–1.0 | Never started unless slack. Already built, so nothing to cut: N-09 (wave 2), N-11 (wave 1), S4 (wave 2); N-04a is prebuilt since wave 5 (no mp4 = off) |
| 2 | K-26 recap endpoint | 0.5 | The director reads numbers off Run → World into the ceremony |
| 3 | K-29 heartbeat card | 0.5 | The tech op walks the tablets each act |
| 4 | ~~K-19 Kokoro on the VPS~~ (moot since wave 2: opt-in) | 0 | The devices' own voices are the default |
| 5 | K-20 house-announcement player | 0.5 | Pre-rendered announcement files played from QLab (P-3) |
| 6 | **K-31b**, iPhone push (conditional anyway; built dormant in wave 5: leave `LOOM_HOMESCREEN` off unless K-31a is green 10-21) | 2.0 | Android + booth push; iPhones keep the app open, screen on |
| 7 | **K-31a**, push entirely | 1.5 | The "push not working" row in §12; build N-09 instead if there's half a day |
| 8 | K-25 ceremony beats | 1.0 | Clippy hosts the awards by hand from a printed script with the trophies; the TV voice is idle (**weakens #26**) |
| 9 | K-30 Quest | 1.5 | W-33 (decided 10-22 anyway) |
| 10 | K-28 measured map | 0.5 | The placeholder map: the game still works, it just isn't this house |
| 11 | K-13 room check-in | 0.75 | Antivirus scan + the 📍 button at summonses (**drops an explicit ask**) |
| 12 | K-18 TV voice | 1.5 | The TV shows the face + slides; QLab plays pre-rendered speeches at the scripted moments; DMs stay text (**weakens #13**) |
| 13 | K-17 overlays + slides | 1.5 | Tablets on attract/auto; the tech op runs the slides on the TV as a Keynote deck by hand |
| 14 | K-24 File a fact | 1.5 | DM lies don't count (**contradicts #33**); hunt answers only in the room |
| 15 | K-21 cue sheet | 0.75 | The raw Events dropdown + the printed cue order |

**Wave 4 (U21, cut in this order, each a revert of its lane):** the **King** first (drop `guard` from both maps and the `king` prop: the door is open in the simulation; ~~the Lichess phone~~ (retired in wave 11) — the Server Door terminal + ♟ are the fallback) → **marked for reinstallation** (the hosts' verdicts back to capture-only; `# quarantine` unused) → **staff** quest logs (the booth rooms off; the story lines stay) → the **Truthcoin wallet** (drop the `currency:` header: ⭐ + the host buttons come back, W-27) → **judging + the bottle** (`Night.judge_mode` `review` with the 📝 buttons; the old hat is not coming back — Clippy reads the truths from `facts.json` herself) → **facts / codex** last (never: the hunt questions live there now).

**Wave 7 (each a switch or a revert):** the **server-room terminal** → don't provision it (Trabolta's candid lines still play in every phone's Server Room and his DM thread; 🕊 can still be pressed on the director's call); ~~**the riddle** → take the sign down~~ (wave 11: no riddle; the sign points at the notes and the King); **The Heart** → nothing to cut (with the contact dead it simply never plays; the director's 🚪 is optional). The keypad lock itself is never cut once fitted: it is real hardware, and egress never depends on it.

**Wave 11 (each a switch or a revert):** the **Server Door terminal** → don't provision it (the digit notes and the majority still give the code; ♟ / `chess_won` for a game a host saw elsewhere); the King's **hits** → nothing to cut (👑 restores him, Set… holds him anywhere); **ghost pictures** → nothing to cut (no picture = the capsule); **reserved programs** → 🔓 each one, or drop the `reserved:` lines (W-03's trust at the door); **guest countdowns** → drop `, for guests`; **arm-then-fire** → drop `arm:`; **the busy line** → drop `busy:`. The digit notes are never cut once printed (take the four cards down if a code leaks).

**Wave 5 (each a switch or a revert):** `LOOM_HOMESCREEN` off (K-31b) and `LOOM_SNAPSHOT_MS` unset are already the off positions; no mp4 = no N-04a; the `!!` takeover → change the two pins back to `broadcast "!GO TO THE LIVING ROOM" to everyone`; Runescape's grant + freeze are not cut (the invitation promised them; the minter role gets them even without the clauses): a wrong freeze is ended with Run → Wallet's ☀ Lift, a wrong grant corrected with 🪙 Charge; the phone cue sheet → don't hand out `?role=director` (the radio).

**Never cut:** K-01 (public HTTPS), K-04 (Trabolta live), K-05 (house link + the safe-light edits), K-06 (alerts), K-07 (test sheet), K-08 (freeze/backups/show-night copies), K-10 (pinned codes), **K-16 (Trabolta hidden on terminals: secrecy, #32)**, **K-23 (hunt-answer secrecy)**, **K-32 (load test)**, N-01 (iPhone door scan), N-02 (re-pair), **N-10 (release a performer)**. If one of these is at risk on 10-23, take time from the bottom of this table.

## 14. Wrap (Sun Nov 1)

1. Backups: `pg_dump` + the `loom-state` tar off the VPS; Run → Export (`loom-run/1`) for the archive (SW-ADM-09, SW-OPS-16).
2. **Change the mod passcode** (W-15) and rotate anything printed.
3. Archive the ceremony script, recap JSON and photos; return the deposit-box items; `lights_restore`; end the event.
4. Tear-down: tent, cords, GFCIs, tablets back to their owners (factory reset borrowed ones).
5. A one-hour retro with the crew → re-plan [`SCHEDULE.md`](./SCHEDULE.md) for the next run: every 🩹 and 💤 item in the checklist is the backlog, and everything built here counts as done.

## 15. How this doc stays honest

- **Tick `🎃` items in the checklist** in the PR that lands them (the usual ✅ rules), and tick the acceptance line in §5 with the device it was checked on.
- **Re-date the checklist's "Last reviewed"** at every gate in §2.
- A cut moves its checklist tag from 🎃 to 🩹 with the fallback named.
- The inputs behind this plan were three working passes on 2026-10-15: a triage of every WP (K-xx ids), a Halifax hardware + production plan (I-, D-, A-, L-, B-, P- ids) and a show-runner walk-through (N-xx, OP-xx). Two conflicts between them were settled here: the Quest software time-box ends 10-20 with the final call at the 10-22 bench gate, and the pre-rendered announcement/Trabolta files are the **fallback**, not the plan, since K-18/K-20 are built.
- **Revised 2026-10-15 evening by two critic passes.**
  - A capacity review, checked against the code: K-31 split into a/b, re-estimates, K-32, merge order + route files, the Push-draft rule, the door-rush limits, client-side print QRs, the `widgetResultArgs` exemption.
  - A show-safety review: N-10, the safe-light YAML, no lock rules, ≤ 3 Hz presets, Lichess level 2–3, the house manager, `ALL STOP` / `ARRIVAL`, OP-18…21, show-night backups + warm standby, and the risk rows for the router, coordinator, director laptop and backup Mac.
  - The committed total went from 26.75 to 27.6 lane-days, plus 2.0 conditional for K-31b.
